📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期乙型肝炎病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 基线分析结果:与普遍接种出生剂量策略(255例儿童感染 [95% 置信区间,130至530])相比,延迟接种和共享决策策略将使2026年出生的队列研究地区的慢性HBV感染人数分别增加19%至303(95% 置信区间,155至610)和83%至467(95% 置信区间,209至1032)。这些策略还将使20年后的HBV易感人群数量分别增加至474 000和1 806 000,相比普遍接种出生剂量策略的232 000。
  • 在180名HCT前anti-HBc阴性的患者中,有6名(3.3%)在HCT后anti-HBc转为阳性。在164名HCT前anti-HBs阴性的患者中,有49名(29.9%)在HCT后anti-HBs转为阳性。所有新检测到抗体的患者均保持HBsAg阴性,且可获得的HBV DNA检测结果未显示有活动性HBV感染的证据。在后续随访检测中,3名可评估的患者在中位数240天后anti-HBc消失,而32名可评估患者中,有30名(93.8%)在中位数119天后anti-HBs水平下降或消失。
  • EP、EN和HC组的α多样性和β多样性均无显著差异。种群水平分析发现,与Bacteroidales和Firmicutes相关的厌氧共生菌存在组间差异,但这些差异在全局FDR校正后均不显著。在EN组中,选定的菌群在下采样、留一法和CLR基础上的敏感性分析中显示出方向一致的关联,但这些发现同样在FDR校正后不显著。
  • HDV-HCC患者年龄较小,肝功能受损更严重,AST、CRP和白细胞水平较低。HDV-HCC肿瘤体积较小,门静脉浸润较少见,且更常符合米兰标准。全身免疫特征分析显示HDV-HCC和HBV-HCC之间存在不同的炎症模式。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • RESULTS OF BASE-CASE ANALYSIS: Compared with the universal birth-dose strategy (255 childhood infections [95% UI, 130 to 530]), the deferred and shared decision-making strategies would increase chronic HBV infections in the 2026 birth cohort by 19% to 303 (95% UI, 155 to 610) and by 83% to 467 (95% UI, 209 to 1032), respectively.
  • Among 180 patients who were anti-HBc negative before HCT, 6 (3.3%) developed anti-HBc positivity after HCT. Among 164 patients who were anti-HBs negative before HCT, 49 (29.9%) developed anti-HBs positivity.
  • Active infection prevalence was 136.2 per 100,000 donors (95% CI, 131.2-141.3) for HBV and 57.4 (95% CI, 53.8-61.1) for HCV.
  • Alpha and beta diversity did not differ significantly among EP, EN, and HC groups.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Journal of Viral Hepatitis  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-01  |  DOI: 10.1111/jvh.70250  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Future scheduled publication  |  Citation: 2026, 33, 11, e70250
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

恩替卡韦与替诺福韦艾拉酚胺在慢性乙型肝炎患者中对肝细胞癌长期发病率的比较:REAL-B研究

Similar Long-Term Incidence of Hepatocellular Carcinoma in Chronic Hepatitis B Initiating Entecavir Versus Tenofovir Alafenamide: A REAL-B Study.
作者： Haruki Uojima, Rui Huang, Huy Ngoc Trinh, Akito Nozaki, Philip Vutien, Hidenori Toyoda, Takashi Honda, Hiroshi Abe, Masanori Atsukawa, Tsunamasa Watanabe
摘要中文翻译关于恩替卡韦(ETV)和替诺福韦艾拉酚胺(TAF)在慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)患者中对肝细胞癌(HCC)发病率潜在差异的数据有限。本回顾性多国研究纳入了2016年以来在4个国家的23个中心开始接受ETV或TAF治疗的、无HCC病史且未接受过抗病毒药物治疗的CHB成人患者。使用逆概率加权(IPTW)和倾向评分匹配(PSM)方法平衡基线特征。采用竞争风险分析方法分析HCC发病率及抗病毒治疗方案与HCC之间的关联。共纳入1437名患者(ETV组710名,TAF组727名),其平均随访时间相似(ETV组55.5 ± 29.8个月 vs. TAF组58.0 ± 25.5个月;p = 0.077)。接受ETV治疗的患者年龄更大,男性比例更高,且更可能患有肝硬化。在IPTW调整后,两组患者的特征趋于平衡(所有标准化差异均<0.1)。调整后随访期间,ETV组有23例患者和TAF组有20例患者发展为HCC,其HCC发病率分别为每100人年0.67和0.65(p = 0.853)。两组之间HCC风险无统计学显著差异(SHR:1.09;95% CI:0.44-2.65;p = 0.851),且在按年龄、性别、肝硬化、脂肪肝、HBeAg状态、基线肝酶水平、HBV DNA水平、糖尿病等亚组分析以及使用PSM进行敏感性分析中结果一致。在这一真实世界多国研究中,对于未接受过抗病毒治疗的CHB患者,ETV和TAF组的HCC发病率无显著差异,提示治疗选择应考虑成本和患者个体因素。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
关于恩替卡韦(ETV)和替诺福韦艾拉酚胺(TAF)在慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)患者中对肝细胞癌(HCC)发病率潜在差异的数据有限。
设计与对象
本回顾性多国研究纳入了2016年以来在4个国家的23个中心开始接受ETV或TAF治疗的、无HCC病史且未接受过抗病毒药物治疗的CHB成人患者。
核心方法
使用逆概率加权(IPTW)和倾向评分匹配(PSM)方法平衡基线特征。
主要结果
共纳入1437名患者(ETV组710名,TAF组727名),其平均随访时间相似(ETV组55.5 ± 29.8个月 vs. TAF组58.0 ± 25.5个月;p = 0.077)。
解释与创新
在IPTW调整后随访期间,ETV组有23例患者和TAF组有20例患者发展为HCC,其HCC发病率分别为每100人年0.67和0.65(p = 0.853)。
科研与公卫意义
两组之间HCC风险无统计学显著差异(SHR:1.09;95% CI:0.44-2.65;p = 0.851),且在按年龄、性别、肝硬化、脂肪肝、HBeAg状态、基线肝酶水平、HBV DNA水平、糖尿病等亚组分析以及使用PSM进行敏感性分析中结果一致。
局限与证据强度
接受ETV治疗的患者年龄更大,男性比例更高,且更可能患有肝硬化。

Similar Long-Term Incidence of Hepatocellular Carcinoma in Chronic Hepatitis B Initiating Entecavir Versus Tenofovir Alafenamide: A REAL-B Study.

Authors: Haruki Uojima, Rui Huang, Huy Ngoc Trinh, Akito Nozaki, Philip Vutien, Hidenori Toyoda, Takashi Honda, Hiroshi Abe, Masanori Atsukawa, Tsunamasa Watanabe
Original abstract (English)
Data on potential differences in hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) incidence between entecavir (ETV) and tenofovir alafenamide (TAF) in chronic hepatitis B (CHB) patients are limited. This retrospective multinational study included treatment-naïve adults with CHB without a history of HCC who initiated ETV or TAF therapy since 2016 at 23 centres in 4 countries. Baseline characteristics were balanced using inverse probability of treatment weighting (IPTW) and propensity score matching (PSM). HCC incidence and the association between antiviral regimen and HCC were analysed using competing-risk analysis. A total of 1437 patients (710 ETV, 727 TAF) were included, with similar mean follow-up durations (55.5 ± 29.8 vs. 58.0 ± 25.5 months; p = 0.077). ETV-treated patients were older, more frequently male, and more likely to have cirrhosis. After IPTW, both groups had balanced characteristics (all standardised differences < 0.1). After IPTW adjustment, during follow-up, 23 patients on ETV and 20 on TAF developed HCC, with incidence rates of 0.67 and 0.65 per 100 person-years, respectively (p = 0.853). There was no statistically significant difference in HCC risk between the groups (SHR: 1.09; 95% CI: 0.44-2.65; p = 0.851), with consistent findings across subgroups by age, sex, cirrhosis, hepatic steatosis, HBeAg, baseline liver enzymes, HBV DNA levels, diabetes mellitus and in sensitivity analysis using PSM. In this real-world multinational study of treatment-naïve CHB patients, HCC incidence did not significantly differ between ETV and TAF groups, suggesting therapeutic selection should consider cost and patient-specific factors.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Data on potential differences in hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) incidence between entecavir (ETV) and tenofovir alafenamide (TAF) in chronic hepatitis B (CHB) patients are limited.
Design and population
This retrospective multinational study included treatment-naïve adults with CHB without a history of HCC who initiated ETV or TAF therapy since 2016 at 23 centres in 4 countries.
Core methods
Baseline characteristics were balanced using inverse probability of treatment weighting (IPTW) and propensity score matching (PSM).
Main results
A total of 1437 patients (710 ETV, 727 TAF) were included, with similar mean follow-up durations (55.5 ± 29.8 vs. 58.0 ± 25.5 months; p = 0.077).
Interpretation and novelty
After IPTW adjustment, during follow-up, 23 patients on ETV and 20 on TAF developed HCC, with incidence rates of 0.67 and 0.65 per 100 person-years, respectively (p = 0.853).
Scientific and public-health significance
There was no statistically significant difference in HCC risk between the groups (SHR: 1.09; 95% CI: 0.44-2.65; p = 0.851), with consistent findings across subgroups by age, sex, cirrhosis, hepatic steatosis, HBeAg, baseline liver enzymes, HBV DNA levels, diabetes mellitus and in sensitivity analysis using PSM.
Limitations and evidence strength
ETV-treated patients were older, more frequently male, and more likely to have cirrhosis.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

GST-HG131在慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)患者中的安全性、耐受性和功能性治愈活性:一项随机、双盲IIa期研究。

Safety, Tolerability and Antiviral Activity of GST-HG131 in Chronic Hepatitis B: A Randomized, Double-Blind Phase IIa Study.
作者： Yuming Guo, Zhe Xu, Yuanyuan Li, John Mao, Tianxiang Zhang, Fanping Meng, Junliang Fu, Dong Ji, Yingjie Ji, Yongqian Cheng
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)仍然是全球主要的健康负担。由于HBsAg清除率低、病毒复制模板——共价闭合环状DNA(共价闭合环状DNA)的持续存在以及需要终身治疗,现有疗法很少能实现功能性治愈。
设计与对象
这项双盲、随机、安慰剂对照的IIa期研究评估了GST-HG131在45名慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)患者中的疗效和安全性。所有参与者均接受了超过6个月的核苷(酸)类似物单药治疗,HBsAg水平在100至1500 IU/mL之间,且血清ALT低于1× ULN。
核心方法
组1和组2(各10名患者;8名接受治疗,2名接受安慰剂)分别接受了30 mg或60 mg每日两次(BID)治疗28天。组3(25名患者;20名接受治疗,5名接受安慰剂)接受了30 mg BID治疗12周。
主要结果
在第28天时,30 mg每日两次28天组的HBsAg水平降低了-0.65 log10 IU/mL,而60 mg每日两次28天组的HBsAg水平降低了-0.53 log10 IU/mL。在治疗结束时,12周组的HBsAg水平比安慰剂组减少了更多的-0.89 log10 IU/mL。值得注意的是,12周组中有76.5%的患者实现了HBsAg低于100 IU/mL。
解释与创新
在12周的治疗后观察到最大的功能性治愈活性,支持进一步开展GST-HG131在慢性乙型肝炎功能性治愈策略中的临床开发。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,GST-HG131可能是一种有前景的候选药物,有助于改善慢性乙型肝炎患者中HBsAg的清除,从而可能为功能性治愈策略做出贡献。
局限与证据强度
该研究样本量较小且治疗时间较短,这可能影响其结果的普遍性。由于研究设计和有限的随访时间,证据强度中等。

Safety, Tolerability and Antiviral Activity of GST-HG131 in Chronic Hepatitis B: A Randomized, Double-Blind Phase IIa Study.

Authors: Yuming Guo, Zhe Xu, Yuanyuan Li, John Mao, Tianxiang Zhang, Fanping Meng, Junliang Fu, Dong Ji, Yingjie Ji, Yongqian Cheng
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND AND AIMS: Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) remains a major global health burden. Current therapies rarely achieve a functional cure due to low rates of HBsAg clearance, the persistence of the viral replication template-covalently closed circular DNA (cccDNA)-and the need for lifelong treatment. GST-HG131 is a first-in-class, orally bioavailable small-molecule inhibitor of PAPD5/7, key host factors involved in HBV mRNA stabilization and HBsAg production. We evaluated the safety, tolerability and antiviral activity of GST-HG131 in patients with CHB receiving nucleos(t)ide analogue (NUC) therapy in a randomized, double-blind, placebo-controlled Phase IIa study. METHODS: This double-blind, randomized, placebo-controlled Phase IIa study evaluated the efficacy and safety of GST-HG131 in 45 CHB patients. Cohorts 1 and 2 (n = 10 each; 8 active, 2 placebo) received 30 mg or 60 mg BID for 28 days, respectively. Cohort 3 (n = 25; 20 active, five placebo) received 30 mg BID for 12 weeks. All participants were on nucleos(t)ide analog monotherapy for more than 6 months, with HBsAg between 100 and 1500 IU/mL, and serum ALT below 1× ULN. RESULTS: At Day 28, HBsAg reductions were -0.65 log10 IU/mL in the 30 mg BID 28-day group and -0.53 log10 IU/mL in the 60 mg BID 28-day group. The 30 mg BID 12-week group showed a greater reduction of -0.89 log10 IU/mL at the end of treatment versus placebo (p 50% reduction in HBsAg with a maximum decrease of 1.64 log10 IU/mL. Notably, 76.5% of patients in the 12-week group achieved HBsAg below 100 IU/mL. Rebounds in HBsAg were observed following treatment discontinuation. GST-HG131 was well tolerated, with no dose-related toxicity or emerging safety concerns across all cohorts. CONCLUSION: GST-HG131, a first-in-class oral PAPD5/7 inhibitor, demonstrated favourable safety and significant HBsAg reduction in patients with CHB. The greatest antiviral activity was observed after 12 weeks of treatment, supporting further clinical development of GST-HG131 as a component of functional cure strategies for CHB. TRIAL REGISTRATION: ClinicalTrials.gov identifier: NCT06263959.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) remains a major global health burden. Current therapies rarely achieve a functional cure due to low rates of HBsAg clearance, the persistence of the viral replication template-covalently closed circular DNA (cccDNA)-and the need for lifelong treatment.
Design and population
This double-blind, randomized, placebo-controlled Phase IIa study evaluated the efficacy and safety of GST-HG131 in 45 CHB patients. All participants were on nucleos(t)ide analog monotherapy for more than 6 months, with HBsAg between 100 and 1500 IU/mL, and serum ALT below 1× ULN.
Core methods
Cohorts 1 and 2 (n = 10 each; 8 active, 2 placebo) received 30 mg or 60 mg BID for 28 days, respectively. Cohort 3 (n = 25; 20 active, five placebo) received 30 mg BID for 12 weeks.
Main results
At Day 28, HBsAg reductions were -0.65 log10 IU/mL in the 30 mg BID 28-day group and -0.53 log10 IU/mL in the 60 mg BID 28-day group. The 30 mg BID 12-week group showed a greater reduction of -0.89 log10 IU/mL at the end of treatment versus placebo. Notably, 76.5% of patients in the 12-week group achieved HBsAg below 100 IU/mL.
Interpretation and novelty
The greatest antiviral activity was observed after 12 weeks of treatment, supporting further clinical development of GST-HG131 as a component of functional cure strategies for CHB.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that GST-HG131 may be a promising candidate for improving HBsAg clearance in patients with chronic hepatitis B, potentially contributing to functional cure strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited to a small sample size and short treatment duration, which may affect generalisability. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the study design and limited follow-up.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

2026年联邦儿童免疫接种计划调整对美国慢性乙型肝炎病毒感染的影响预测

Projected Effect of 2026 Federal Changes to the Childhood Immunization Schedule on Chronic Hepatitis B Virus Infections in the United States.
作者： Amir M. Mohareb, Lakshmi Ganapathi, Caitlin M. Dugdale, Ruma Rajbhandari, Andrea L. Ciaranello, Anne M. Neilan, Ravi R. Jhaveri, Arthur Y. Kim, Emily Parker Hyle
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
美国最近提出了对儿童乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)疫苗接种接种建议的调整。为了预测如果完全实施疫苗政策调整,对慢性HBV感染的影响。
设计与对象
一种马尔可夫乙型肝炎病毒母婴传播和儿童感染状态转移模型被采用。目标人群为美国出生婴儿的一岁出生(n = 3.6百万)。时间范围为20年。
核心方法
数据来源:发表的关于母亲HBV 患病率(0.4%)和乙肝表面抗原检测(99.5%灵敏度)的数据,儿童疫苗接种完成率(普遍出生剂量策略:91.1%;延迟策略:87.1%;共享决策策略:50.0%),以及按年龄分层的慢性HBV感染风险。
主要结果
基线分析结果:与普遍接种出生剂量策略(255例儿童感染 [95% 置信区间,130至530])相比,延迟接种和共享决策策略将使2026年出生的队列研究地区的慢性HBV感染人数分别增加19%至303(95% 置信区间,155至610)和83%至467(95% 置信区间,209至1032)。这些策略还将使20年后的HBV易感人群数量分别增加至474 000和1 806 000,相比普遍接种出生剂量策略的232 000。
解释与创新
通过共享决策进行的疫苗接种接种政策调整预计会导致儿童HBV感染增加83%,并且每年有50%至70%的儿童进入成年时仍对HBV感染易感。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,疫苗接种接种政策的调整可能显著影响美国人群中慢性HBV感染的患病率和HBV感染的易感性,为未来的公共卫生策略和疫苗接种建议提供信息。
局限与证据强度
水平HBV传播风险的不确定性限制了预测的可信度。证据强度为中等,因为其依赖于已发表的数据和概率敏感性分析。

Projected Effect of 2026 Federal Changes to the Childhood Immunization Schedule on Chronic Hepatitis B Virus Infections in the United States.

Authors: Amir M. Mohareb, Lakshmi Ganapathi, Caitlin M. Dugdale, Ruma Rajbhandari, Andrea L. Ciaranello, Anne M. Neilan, Ravi R. Jhaveri, Arthur Y. Kim, Emily Parker Hyle
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: The United States recently proposed changes to the recommendations for childhood hepatitis B virus (HBV) vaccination. OBJECTIVE: To project the effect of vaccine policy changes, if fully implemented, on chronic HBV infections. DESIGN: Markov cohort state transition model of perinatal and childhood HBV infection. DATA SOURCES: Published data on maternal HBV prevalence (0.4%) and hepatitis B surface antigen testing (99.5% sensitivity), childhood vaccine completion (universal birth-dose strategy: 91.1%; deferred strategy: 87.1%; shared decision-making strategy: 50.0%), and age-stratified risk for chronic HBV infection. TARGET POPULATION: One-year birth cohort of U.S.-born infants (n = 3.6 million). TIME HORIZON: 20 years. INTERVENTION: Universal birth-dose vaccination, deferred (2-month) vaccination, and vaccination through shared decision making. OUTCOME MEASURES: Numbers with chronic HBV infection and susceptibility to HBV, with 95% uncertainty intervals (UIs) derived from probabilistic sensitivity analysis. RESULTS OF BASE-CASE ANALYSIS: Compared with the universal birth-dose strategy (255 childhood infections [95% UI, 130 to 530]), the deferred and shared decision-making strategies would increase chronic HBV infections in the 2026 birth cohort by 19% to 303 (95% UI, 155 to 610) and by 83% to 467 (95% UI, 209 to 1032), respectively. These strategies would also increase the number of HBV-susceptible people at year 20 (474 000 and 1 806 000) compared with the universal birth-dose strategy (232 000). RESULTS OF SENSITIVITY ANALYSIS: In a scenario with lower vaccine coverage (84.5%) and higher HBV prevalence (maternal prevalence of 0.72% and higher risk for horizontal infection), chronic HBV infections would increase to 1700 and 2192, respectively, with the deferred and shared decision-making strategies. LIMITATION: Uncertainty in horizontal HBV infection risk. CONCLUSION: Proposed policy changes for vaccination through shared decision making are projected to result in an 83% increase in childhood HBV infections, and between 50% and 70% of children entering adulthood annually would be susceptible to HBV infection. Policymakers should reimplement recommendations for universal birth-dose vaccination. PRIMARY FUNDING SOURCE: National Institute of Allergy and Infectious Diseases.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The United States recently proposed changes to the recommendations for childhood hepatitis B virus (HBV) vaccination. To project the effect of vaccine policy changes, if fully implemented, on chronic HBV infections.
Design and population
A Markov cohort state transition model of perinatal and childhood HBV infection was used. The target population was one-year birth cohort of U.S.-born infants (n = 3.6 million). The time horizon was 20 years.
Core methods
DATA SOURCES: Published data on maternal HBV prevalence (0.4%) and hepatitis B surface antigen testing (99.5% sensitivity), childhood vaccine completion (universal birth-dose strategy: 91.1%; deferred strategy: 87.1%; shared decision-making strategy: 50.0%), and age-stratified risk for chronic HBV infection.
Main results
RESULTS OF BASE-CASE ANALYSIS: Compared with the universal birth-dose strategy (255 childhood infections [95% UI, 130 to 530]), the deferred and shared decision-making strategies would increase chronic HBV infections in the 2026 birth cohort by 19% to 303 (95% UI, 155 to 610) and by 83% to 467 (95% UI, 209 to 1032), respectively. These strategies would also increase the number of HBV-susceptible people at year 20 (474 000 and 1 806 000) compared with the universal birth-dose strategy (232 000).
Interpretation and novelty
Proposed policy changes for vaccination through shared decision making are projected to result in an 83% increase in childhood HBV infections, and between 50% and 70% of children entering adulthood annually would be susceptible to HBV infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that changes in vaccination policy could significantly impact the prevalence of chronic HBV infections and susceptibility to HBV in the U.S. population, informing future public health strategies and vaccine recommendations.
Limitations and evidence strength
Uncertainty in horizontal HBV infection risk limits confidence in the projections. The evidence is moderate as it relies on published data and probabilistic sensitivity analysis.

巴西血库献血者中乙型和丙型肝炎的长期时间趋势:患病率、发病率及人口统计学相关因素,来自REDS多中心计划。

Long-term temporal trends in hepatitis B and C among blood donors in Brazil: Prevalence, incidence, and demographic correlates from the REDS multicenter program.
作者： André Rolim Belisário, Paulo Jeng Chian Suen, Isabelle Rocha de Oliveira, Teresa Cristina Abreu de Ferrari, Maisa Aparecida Ribeiro, Carolina Miranda, Márcio K. Oikawa, Luiz Amorim, Edyla Almeida, Allyson Guimarães da Costa
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
病毒性肝炎仍然是一个重大的公共卫生挑战。我们评估了巴西五个血库中心献血者中乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)患病率和丙型肝炎病毒(HCV)患病率、发病率以及感染力(FOI)的长期趋势,并探讨了相关的人口统计学因素。
设计与对象
分析了NHLBI受体流行病学和供体评估研究计划的数据。对HBV的2,062,816名首次献血者(2008-2023年)和HCV的1,673,745名首次献血者(2012-2023年)估计了患病率。对HBV的1,346,256名重复献血者和HCV的1,151,209名重复献血者估计了发病率。
核心方法
历史感染力(FOI)使用贝叶斯血清催化模型进行估计。活动性HBV感染定义为反应性HBsAg和抗-HBc或反应性HBV核酸检测(NAT),活动性HCV感染定义为反应性NAT。
主要结果
Active infection prevalence was 136.2 per 100,000 donors (95% CI, 131.2-141.3) for HBV and 57.4 (95% CI, 53.8-61.1) for HCV. Incidence rates were 3.54 (95% CI, 3.01-4.11) and 3.99 (95% CI, 3.33-4.72) per 100,000 person-years, respectively. Prevalence decreased annually by 7.6% for HBV and 11.0% for HCV. Higher prevalence and incidence were associated with male sex, older age, lower educational attainment, and Black or mixed-race skin color. Regional differences persisted, and FOI declined markedly for both infections.
解释与创新
HBV和HCV的患病率、发病率以及FOI在巴西献血者中显著下降,表明传播有所减少,尽管改善的献血者筛选和检测也可能有所贡献。
科研与公卫意义
持续的人口统计学和区域差异支持继续进行监测和针对性预防。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计或证据强度的具体限制。

Long-term temporal trends in hepatitis B and C among blood donors in Brazil: Prevalence, incidence, and demographic correlates from the REDS multicenter program.

Authors: André Rolim Belisário, Paulo Jeng Chian Suen, Isabelle Rocha de Oliveira, Teresa Cristina Abreu de Ferrari, Maisa Aparecida Ribeiro, Carolina Miranda, Márcio K. Oikawa, Luiz Amorim, Edyla Almeida, Allyson Guimarães da Costa
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Viral hepatitis remains a major public health challenge. We evaluated long-term trends in hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis C virus (HCV) prevalence, incidence, and force of infection (FOI) among blood donors from five Brazilian blood centers and investigated associated demographic factors. STUDY DESIGN AND METHODS: Data from the NHLBI Recipient Epidemiology and Donor Evaluation Study program were analyzed. Prevalence was estimated among 2,062,816 first-time donors for HBV (2008-2023) and 1,673,745 for HCV (2012-2023). Incidence was estimated among 1,346,256 repeat donors for HBV and 1,151,209 for HCV. Active HBV infection was defined by reactive HBsAg and anti-HBc or reactive HBV nucleic acid testing (NAT), and active HCV infection by reactive NAT. Historical FOI was estimated using a Bayesian serocatalytic model. RESULTS: Active infection prevalence was 136.2 per 100,000 donors (95% CI, 131.2-141.3) for HBV and 57.4 (95% CI, 53.8-61.1) for HCV. Incidence rates were 3.54 (95% CI, 3.01-4.11) and 3.99 (95% CI, 3.33-4.72) per 100,000 person-years, respectively. Prevalence decreased annually by 7.6% for HBV and 11.0% for HCV. Higher prevalence and incidence were associated with male sex, older age, lower educational attainment, and Black or mixed-race skin color. Regional differences persisted, and FOI declined markedly for both infections. DISCUSSION: HBV and HCV prevalence, incidence, and FOI declined substantially among Brazilian blood donors, suggesting reduced transmission, although improved donor selection and screening may also have contributed. Persistent demographic and regional disparities support continued surveillance and targeted prevention.

摘要 C042:美国成年人对乙型肝炎病毒和丙型肝炎病毒作为癌症病因的认知:来自HINTS 7的研究结果

Abstract C042: Awareness of hepatitis B and hepatitis C viruses as causes of cancer among U.S. adults: Findings from HINTS 7
作者： Rachel A. Wattick, Kelly D. Blake, Alana G. Hudson
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景:慢性丙型肝炎病毒(HCV)和乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染是肝细胞癌(HCC)的主要病因,但公众对这些感染可能导致癌症的认知仍不明确。我们研究了美国成年人中对HCV和HBV作为癌症病因的认知的社会人口学预测因素。方法:数据来源于2024年健康信息国家趋势调查(HINTS 7),这是美国全国代表性调查。两个独立的加权多变量逻辑回归模型评估了正确识别HCV和HBV作为癌症病因的因素。分析采用了jackknife复制权重,并调整了年龄、性别、种族/民族、教育程度、家庭收入、农村地区居住、阿巴拉契亚地区居住、健康保险状况、自我评价健康状况和癌症史。每个模型均采用完整案例分析。结果:样本包括7,278名美国成年人。加权后,50.8%为男性,48.4%为女性,60.6%为非西班牙裔白人,87.0%居住在城市地区,91.4%拥有健康保险。约16.7%正确识别HCV为癌症病因,18.0%正确识别HBV为癌症病因。农村地区居住、阿巴拉契亚地区居住、性别、种族/民族、保险状况和癌症史均未独立与认知相关。结论:全国范围内对HCV和HBV作为癌症病因的认知较低,不到五分之一的美国成年人正确识别这些关联。较低的教育水平和较大的年龄与较低的认知相关,突显了HCC预防和病毒性肝炎教育的重点人群。引用格式:Rachel A. 美国成年人对乙型肝炎病毒和丙型肝炎病毒作为癌症病因的认知:来自HINTS 7的研究结果 [摘要]。载于:第19届AACR癌症健康差异科学会议论文集;2026年10月18-21日;佐治亚州亚特兰大。费城(PA):AACR;Cancer Epidemiol Biomarkers Prev 2026;35(10 Suppl):摘要编号C042。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
慢性丙型肝炎病毒(HCV)和乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染是肝细胞癌(HCC)的主要病因,但公众对这些感染可能导致癌症的认知仍不明确。
设计与对象
我们使用2024年健康信息国家趋势调查(HINTS 7)的数据,研究了美国成年人中对HCV和HBV作为癌症病因的认知的社会人口学预测因素,HINTS 7是一项具有全国代表性的调查。
核心方法
两个独立的加权多变量逻辑回归模型评估了正确识别HCV和HBV作为癌症病因的因素。分析采用了jackknife复制权重,并调整了年龄、性别、种族/民族、教育程度、家庭收入、农村地区居住、阿巴拉契亚地区居住、健康保险状况、自我评价健康状况和癌症史。每个模型均采用完整案例分析。
主要结果
样本包括7,278名美国成年人。加权后,50.8%为男性,48.4%为女性,60.6%为非西班牙裔白人,87.0%居住在城市地区,91.4%拥有健康保险。约16.7%正确识别HCV为癌症病因,18.0%正确识别HBV为癌症病因。农村地区居住、阿巴拉契亚地区居住、性别、种族/民族、保险状况和癌症史均未独立与认知相关。
解释与创新
较低的教育水平和较大的年龄与较低的认知相关,突显了HCC预防和病毒性肝炎教育的重点人群。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调了需要有针对性的教育努力以提高对HBV和HCV作为癌症病因的认知,尤其是在教育水平较低和年龄较大的人群中。
局限与证据强度
该研究受限于依赖自我报告数据和使用完整案例分析,这可能引入偏差。由于这些限制,证据强度中等。

Abstract C042: Awareness of hepatitis B and hepatitis C viruses as causes of cancer among U.S. adults: Findings from HINTS 7

Authors: Rachel A. Wattick, Kelly D. Blake, Alana G. Hudson
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background: Chronic hepatitis C virus (HCV) and hepatitis B virus (HBV) infections are major causes of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC), yet public awareness that these infections can cause cancer remains poorly characterized. We examined sociodemographic predictors of awareness that HCV and HBV can cause cancer among U.S. Methods: Data were analyzed from the 2024 Health Information National Trends Survey (HINTS 7), a nationally representative survey of U.S. Two separate survey-weighted multivariable logistic regression models assessed factors associated with correctly identifying HCV and HBV as causes of cancer. Analyses incorporated jackknife replicate weights and adjusted for age, sex, race/ethnicity, education, household income, rurality, Appalachian residence, health insurance status, self-rated health, and cancer history. Complete-case analysis was used for each model. Results: The sample included 7,278 U.S. After weighting, 50.8% were male, 48.4% were female, 60.6% were non-Hispanic White, 87.0% resided in urban areas, and 91.4% had health insurance. Approximately 16.7% correctly identified HCV as a cause of cancer, and 18.0% correctly identified HBV as a cause of cancer. Rurality, Appalachian residence, sex, race/ethnicity, insurance status, and cancer history were not independently associated with awareness. Conclusions: Awareness that HCV and HBV can cause cancer was low nationally, with fewer than one in five U.S. adults correctly identifying these associations. Lower educational attainment and older age were associated with lower awareness, highlighting priority populations for HCC prevention and viral hepatitis education efforts. Citation Format: Rachel A. Awareness of hepatitis B and hepatitis C viruses as causes of cancer among U.S. adults: Findings from HINTS 7 [abstract]. In: Proceedings of the 19th AACR Conference on the Science of Cancer Health Disparities; 2026 Oct 18-21; Atlanta, GA. Philadelphia (PA): AACR; Cancer Epidemiol Biomarkers Prev 2026;35(10 Suppl):Abstract nr C042.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic hepatitis C virus (HCV) and hepatitis B virus (HBV) infections are major causes of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC), yet public awareness that these infections can cause cancer remains poorly characterized.
Design and population
We examined sociodemographic predictors of awareness that HCV and HBV can cause cancer among U.S. adults using data from the 2024 Health Information National Trends Survey (HINTS 7), a nationally representative survey.
Core methods
Two separate survey-weighted multivariable logistic regression models assessed factors associated with correctly identifying HCV and HBV as causes of cancer. Analyses incorporated jackknife replicate weights and adjusted for age, sex, race/ethnicity, education, household income, rurality, Appalachian residence, health insurance status, self-rated health, and cancer history. Complete-case analysis was used for each model.
Main results
The sample included 7,278 U.S. After weighting, 50.8% were male, 48.4% were female, 60.6% were non-Hispanic White, 87.0% resided in urban areas, and 91.4% had health insurance. Approximately 16.7% correctly identified HCV as a cause of cancer, and 18.0% correctly identified HBV as a cause of cancer. Rurality, Appalachian residence, sex, race/ethnicity, insurance status, and cancer history were not independently associated with awareness.
Interpretation and novelty
Lower educational attainment and older age were associated with lower awareness, highlighting priority populations for HCC prevention and viral hepatitis education efforts.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings underscore the need for targeted education efforts to improve awareness of HBV and HCV as causes of cancer, particularly among populations with lower educational attainment and older age groups.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on self-reported data and the use of complete-case analysis, which may introduce bias. The evidence is moderate in strength due to these limitations.

异基因造血细胞移植后乙型肝炎病毒抗体的被动获得

Passive acquisition of hepatitis B virus antibodies after allogeneic hematopoietic cell transplantation.
作者： Takaaki Konuma, Maki Monna-Oiwa, Etsuko Nagai, Seiko Kato, Shohei Andoh, Junichi Kuroda, Satoshi Takahashi, Yasuhito Nannya, Maki Monna‐Oiwa
摘要中文翻译背景:异基因造血细胞移植(HCT)后出现新的乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)血清学阳性结果可能反映的是从血源性产品中被动获得的抗体,而非HBV感染。我们研究了异基因HCT后乙型肝炎表面抗体(anti-HBs)和乙型肝炎核心抗体(anti-HBc)的获得情况及其时间模式。研究设计与方法:我们回顾性评估了2012年5月至2025年3月期间在日本一家机构接受异基因HCT治疗血液系统疾病的一组193名成人患者。在HCT前后检测了anti-HBs、anti-HBc和乙型肝炎表面抗原(HBsAg)。结果:在180名HCT前anti-HBc阴性的患者中,有6名(3.3%)在HCT后anti-HBc转为阳性。在164名HCT前anti-HBs阴性的患者中,有49名(29.9%)在HCT后anti-HBs转为阳性。所有新检测到抗体的患者均保持HBsAg阴性,且可获得的HBV DNA检测结果未显示有活动性HBV感染的证据。在后续随访检测中,3名可评估的患者在中位数240天后anti-HBc消失,而32名可评估患者中,有30名(93.8%)在中位数119天后anti-HBs水平下降或消失。在未接受新鲜冰冻血浆或静脉注射免疫球蛋白的患者中,HCT后anti-HBs浓度显著升高(p< .0001),且接受≥24次血小板输注的患者比接受≤23次的患者升高更明显(p = .0437)。讨论:在无病毒学证据显示感染的情况下,HCT后新出现的HBV抗体较为常见,提示抗体可能通过细胞血制品被动转移,尤其是血小板。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
异基因造血细胞移植(HCT)后出现新的乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)血清学阳性结果可能反映的是从血源性产品中被动获得的抗体,而非HBV感染。
设计与对象
我们研究了2012年5月至2025年3月期间在日本一家机构接受异基因HCT治疗血液系统疾病的一组193名成人患者中,乙型肝炎表面抗体(anti-HBs)和乙型肝炎核心抗体(anti-HBc)的获得情况及其时间模式。
核心方法
在HCT前后检测了anti-HBs、anti-HBc和乙型肝炎表面抗原(HBsAg),并进行随访检测以评估抗体的持续时间。
主要结果
在180名HCT前anti-HBc阴性的患者中,有6名(3.3%)在HCT后anti-HBc转为阳性。在164名HCT前anti-HBs阴性的患者中,有49名(29.9%)在HCT后anti-HBs转为阳性。所有新检测到抗体的患者均保持HBsAg阴性,且可获得的HBV DNA检测结果未显示有活动性HBV感染的证据。在后续随访检测中,3名可评估的患者在中位数240天后anti-HBc消失,而32名可评估患者中,有30名(93.8%)在中位数119天后anti-HBs水平下降或消失。
解释与创新
在无病毒学证据显示感染的情况下,HCT后新出现的HBV抗体较为常见,提示抗体可能通过细胞血制品被动转移,尤其是血小板。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调了在移植后患者中区分被动抗体转移与主动HBV感染的重要性,这对准确评估诊断和监测HBV状态具有重要意义。
局限与证据强度
本研究为回顾性研究,基于日本一家机构的数据,这可能限制其普遍适用性。由于缺乏所有病例的直接病毒学确认,证据强度中等。

Passive acquisition of hepatitis B virus antibodies after allogeneic hematopoietic cell transplantation.

Authors: Takaaki Konuma, Maki Monna-Oiwa, Etsuko Nagai, Seiko Kato, Shohei Andoh, Junichi Kuroda, Satoshi Takahashi, Yasuhito Nannya, Maki Monna‐Oiwa
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Newly positive hepatitis B virus (HBV) serologic results after allogeneic hematopoietic cell transplantation (HCT) may reflect passive antibody transfer from blood-derived products rather than HBV infection. We investigated the incidence and temporal patterns of hepatitis B surface antibody (anti-HBs) and antibody to hepatitis B core antibody (anti-HBc) acquisition after allogeneic HCT. STUDY DESIGN AND METHODS: We retrospectively evaluated 193 adults who underwent allogeneic HCT for hematologic diseases between May 2012 and March 2025 at a single institution in Japan. Anti-HBs, anti-HBc, and hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) were assessed before and after HCT. RESULTS: Among 180 patients who were anti-HBc negative before HCT, 6 (3.3%) developed anti-HBc positivity after HCT. Among 164 patients who were anti-HBs negative before HCT, 49 (29.9%) developed anti-HBs positivity. All patients with newly detectable antibodies remained HBsAg negative, and available HBV DNA measurements showed no evidence of active HBV infection. Among patients with subsequent follow-up measurements, anti-HBc disappeared in 3 of 3 evaluable patients after a median of 240 days, whereas anti-HBs declined or disappeared in 30 of 32 evaluable patients after a median of 119 days. Among patients receiving neither fresh frozen plasma nor intravenous immunoglobulin, anti-HBs concentrations increased significantly after HCT (p< .0001), with a greater increase among those receiving ≥24 versus ≤23 platelet transfusions (p = .0437). DISCUSSION: Newly detectable HBV antibodies were common after HCT without virologic evidence of infection, suggesting passive antibody transfer from cellular blood components, particularly platelets.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Newly positive hepatitis B virus (HBV) serologic results after allogeneic hematopoietic cell transplantation (HCT) may reflect passive antibody transfer from blood-derived products rather than HBV infection.
Design and population
We investigated the incidence and temporal patterns of hepatitis B surface antibody (anti-HBs) and antibody to hepatitis B core antibody (anti-HBc) acquisition after allogeneic HCT in 193 adults who underwent the procedure for hematologic diseases between May 2012 and March 2025 at a single institution in Japan.
Core methods
Anti-HBs, anti-HBc, and hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) were assessed before and after HCT, with follow-up measurements to evaluate antibody persistence.
Main results
Among 180 patients who were anti-HBc negative before HCT, 6 (3.3%) developed anti-HBc positivity after HCT. Among 164 patients who were anti-HBs negative before HCT, 49 (29.9%) developed anti-HBs positivity. All patients with newly detectable antibodies remained HBsAg negative, and available HBV DNA measurements showed no evidence of active HBV infection. Among patients with subsequent follow-up measurements, anti-HBc disappeared in 3 of 3 evaluable patients after a median of 240 days, whereas anti-HBs declined or disappeared in 30 of 32 evaluable patients after a median of 119 days.
Interpretation and novelty
Newly detectable HBV antibodies were common after HCT without virologic evidence of infection, suggesting passive antibody transfer from cellular blood components, particularly platelets.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings highlight the importance of distinguishing between passive antibody transfer and active HBV infection in post-transplant patients, which has implications for accurate diagnosis and monitoring of HBV status.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is retrospective and based on a single institution in Japan, which may limit generalizability. The evidence is moderate due to the reliance on serologic data without direct virologic confirmation in all cases.

不同的免疫特征定义了与乙型肝炎病毒相关和与丁型肝炎病毒相关的肝细胞癌。

Distinct immune signatures define hepatitis D- versus hepatitis B-associated hepatocellular carcinoma.
作者： Simon Glimmann, Katja Deterding, Kilian Bock, Birgit Bremer, Anke R. M. Kraft, Markus Cornberg, Heiner H. Wedemeyer, Bernd Heinrich, Anika Wranke
摘要中文翻译背景:肝细胞癌(HCC)是全球癌症相关死亡的主要原因之一。丁型肝炎病毒(HDV)是病毒性肝炎中最严重的形式。然而,与HDV相关的HCC的临床、病毒学和全身炎症特征仍不够明确。方法:本研究为单中心回顾性研究,纳入了162例因慢性乙型肝炎(HBV-HCC;n=108)或慢性丁型肝炎(HDV-HCC;n=54)导致的HCC患者。使用邻近扩展检测法(proximity extension assay)对HCC患者的血浆中92种可溶性炎症介质(SIMs)水平进行了定量分析,包括50例HBV-HCC、30例HDV-HCC以及匹配的非HCC对照组(HBV=19,HDV=17)。结果:在HCC诊断时,慢性乙型肝炎患者年龄较小,肝合成和解毒功能受损更严重,AST、CRP和白细胞水平较低。HDV-HCC肿瘤体积较小,门静脉浸润较少见,且更常符合米兰标准(所有p<0.01)。全身免疫特征分析显示两种病因的HCC具有不同的炎症模式。与HBV-HCC相比,HDV-HCC表现出更强的IL-10相关免疫调节特征,而HBV-HCC则表现出更显著的促炎特征,涉及IL-6和磷酸化及增殖相关信号。尽管存在这些差异,各组的无事件生存率没有显著差异。结论:HBV-HCC更常表现为晚期肿瘤阶段,而HDV-HCC则与更严重的潜在肝病但较小的肿瘤相关。重要的是,HBV相关和HDV相关的HCC表现出不同的全身炎症特征,这可能反映了两种病因之间系统性免疫调节的差异。这些发现可能对生物标志物的开发和未来针对病因的治疗策略具有重要意义。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在描述与丁型肝炎病毒相关和与乙型肝炎病毒相关的肝细胞癌(HCC)的免疫特征,以解决对HDV相关HCC理解不足的问题。
设计与对象
本研究为单中心回顾性研究,纳入了162例HCC患者,其中54例为慢性丁型肝炎(HDV-HCC),108例为慢性乙型肝炎(HBV-HCC)。
核心方法
使用邻近扩展检测法(proximity extension assay)对HCC患者的血浆中92种可溶性炎症介质水平进行了定量分析,包括50例HBV-HCC、30例HDV-HCC以及匹配的非HCC对照组。
主要结果
HDV-HCC患者年龄较小,肝功能受损更严重,AST、CRP和白细胞水平较低。HDV-HCC肿瘤体积较小,门静脉浸润较少见,且更常符合米兰标准。全身免疫特征分析显示HDV-HCC和HBV-HCC之间存在不同的炎症模式。
解释与创新
HDV-HCC表现出更强的IL-10相关免疫调节特征,而HBV-HCC则表现出更显著的促炎特征。这些发现表明两种病因之间存在系统性免疫调节的差异。
科研与公卫意义
HDV-HCC和HBV-HCC不同的全身炎症特征可能对生物标志物的开发和针对病因的治疗策略具有重要意义。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于其单中心设计和回顾性性质,这可能影响结果的普遍性。HDV-HCC的样本量相对较小,且缺乏随机化可能导致偏差。

Distinct immune signatures define hepatitis D- versus hepatitis B-associated hepatocellular carcinoma.

Authors: Simon Glimmann, Katja Deterding, Kilian Bock, Birgit Bremer, Anke R. M. Kraft, Markus Cornberg, Heiner H. Wedemeyer, Bernd Heinrich, Anika Wranke
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) is a leading cause of cancer-related mortality worldwide. Hepatitis D is the most severe form of viral hepatitis. However, the clinical, virological, and systemic inflammatory characteristics of HDV-associated HCC remain insufficiently characterized. METHODS: In this single-center retrospective study, we identified 162 patients with HCC due to chronic hepatitis D (HDV-HCC; n=54) or chronic hepatitis B (HBV-HCC; n=108). Plasma levels of 92 soluble inflammatory mediators (SIMs) were quantified using proximity extension assay in HCC patients [HBV-HCC (n=50), HDV-HCC (n=30), and matched non-HCC controls (HBV=19, HDV=17)]. RESULTS: At the time of HCC diagnosis, patients with CHD were younger, had more impaired liver synthesis and detoxification function, and lower levels of AST, CRP, and leukocytes. HDV-HCCs presented with smaller tumors, less frequent portal vein infiltration, and more often fulfilled Milan criteria (all p<0.01). Systemic immune profiling revealed distinct inflammatory patterns between both etiologies. Compared with HBV-HCC, HDV-HCC was characterized by a comparatively stronger IL-10-associated immunoregulatory signature, whereas HBV-HCC exhibited a more pronounced pro-inflammatory profile involving IL-6- and phosphorylation- and proliferation-associated signaling. Despite these differences, event-free survival did not differ between groups. CONCLUSIONS: HBV-HCC more frequently presented with advanced tumor stage, whereas HDV-HCC was associated with more severe underlying liver disease but smaller tumors. Importantly, HBV-associated and HDV-associated HCC exhibit distinct systemic inflammatory signatures, potentially reflecting differences in systemic immune regulation between etiologies. These findings may have implications for biomarker development and future etiology-specific therapeutic strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to characterize the distinct immune signatures of hepatitis D- versus hepatitis B-associated hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) to address the insufficient understanding of HDV-associated HCC.
Design and population
A single-center retrospective study included 162 patients with HCC, 54 with chronic hepatitis D (HDV-HCC) and 108 with chronic hepatitis B (HBV-HCC).
Core methods
Plasma levels of 92 soluble inflammatory mediators were quantified using proximity extension assay in HCC patients, including 50 HBV-HCC, 30 HDV-HCC, and matched non-HCC controls.
Main results
HDV-HCC patients were younger, had more impaired liver function, and lower levels of AST, CRP, and leukocytes. HDV-HCC tumors were smaller, less frequently infiltrated, and more often fulfilled Milan criteria. Systemic immune profiling revealed distinct inflammatory patterns between HDV-HCC and HBV-HCC.
Interpretation and novelty
HDV-HCC was characterized by a stronger IL-10-associated immunoregulatory signature, while HBV-HCC exhibited a more pronounced pro-inflammatory profile. These findings suggest differences in systemic immune regulation between the two etiologies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The distinct systemic inflammatory signatures of HDV-HCC and HBV-HCC may have implications for biomarker development and etiology-specific therapeutic strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its single-center design and retrospective nature, which may affect generalizability. The sample size for HDV-HCC is relatively small, and the lack of randomization may introduce bias.

江苏省医疗和健康机构乙型肝炎综合预防与控制服务能力调查:一项横断面研究

Comprehensive Prevention and Control Service Capacity for Hepatitis B in Medical and Health Institutions: A Cross-Sectional Survey in Jiangsu Province, China
作者： Quan Kong, Lingen Shi, Jie Jiang, Xiaoyan Liu, Yuheng Chen, Dandan Yang, Yang Dandan
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估江苏省医疗和健康机构提供全面HBV预防与控制服务的能力,以弥补中国在HBV预防方面取得进展后,病例报告、转诊、病毒检测和随访管理方面的差距。
设计与对象
在江苏省的13个地级市开展了横断面调查,涵盖183家医疗和健康机构,包括135家医院和48家疾病预防控制中心。
核心方法
调查评估了HBsAg检测、HBV-DNA检测、病例报告、转诊、病毒检测和随访管理,以及疾病预防控制中心(CDC)实施的公共卫生预防与控制措施。
主要结果
在被调查的183家机构中,86.9%报告具备进行HBsAg检测的能力,而HBV-DNA检测在56.8%的机构中可用,随访管理在34.1%的医院中实施,主要集中在三级医院。
解释与创新
研究发现,尽管HBV实验室检测能力,尤其是HBsAg检测能力较强,但HBV病毒检测和随访服务仍高度集中在三级医院,表明基层机构在长期HBV管理中的参与度有限。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,未来需要加强HBV预防与控制服务链,统一临床诊断标准,增强转诊机制,扩大基层随访能力,并促进医疗机构与CDC之间的协调信息共享。
局限与证据强度
调查依赖于自我报告数据,可能引入偏倚,且HBV病毒检测和随访服务在三级医院的高度集中限制了研究结果在基层机构中的普遍适用性。

Comprehensive Prevention and Control Service Capacity for Hepatitis B in Medical and Health Institutions: A Cross-Sectional Survey in Jiangsu Province, China

Authors: Quan Kong, Lingen Shi, Jie Jiang, Xiaoyan Liu, Yuheng Chen, Dandan Yang, Yang Dandan
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Despite substantial progress in hepatitis B virus (HBV) prevention in China, important gaps remain in diagnosis, referral, treatment, and long-term follow-up. To further strengthen viral hepatitis prevention and control, the National Administration of Disease Control and Prevention formulated the Action Plan for the Prevention and Control of Viral Hepatitis in China (2025–2030) in 2025. Jiangsu Province is classified as a region with moderate endemicity of HBV in China. The capacity of medical and health institutions to deliver comprehensive HBV prevention and control services is essential for effective implementation of HBV control strategies. This study aimed to assess the capacity of medical and health institutions in Jiangsu Province across key components of the HBV prevention and control service chain, including testing, diagnosis reporting, referral, antiviral treatment and follow-up management. Methods A cross-sectional survey was conducted across all 13 prefecture-level cities in Jiangsu Province, encompassing 183 medical and health institutions—comprising 135 hospitals and 48 disease control and prevention centers (CDCs). The survey encompassed the following core items: Hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) testing, HBV-DNA testing, case diagnosis, case referral, antiviral treatment, patient follow-up, and public health prevention and control initiatives implemented by CDCs. Results Among the 183 surveyed institutions, 86.9% (159/183) reported having the capacity to perform HBsAg testing. In 2025, a total of 3.75 million HBsAg tests had been completed, over 90% of which were carried out in tertiary hospitals. HBV-DNA testing was available in 56.8% (104/183) of the surveyed institutions, and all primary hospitals offering this service outsource testing to independent clinical laboratories. Case referral services were provided by 37.2% (68/183) of the institutions, with antiviral treatment resources highly concentrated in tertiary hospitals. Follow-up management was implemented in 34.1% (46/135) of hospitals, mainly among tertiary hospitals. Conclusion Medical and health institutions in Jiangsu Province have established relatively strong capacity for HBV laboratory testing, particularly for HBsAg testing. However, HBV treatment and follow-up services remain highly concentrated in tertiary hospitals, indicating limited participation of primary-level institutions in long-term HBV management. Future efforts should focus on strengthening the HBV prevention and control service chain by unifying clinical diagnostic criteria, enhancing referral mechanisms, expanding grassroots follow-up capacity, and promoting coordinated information sharing between healthcare institutions and CDCs.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the capacity of medical and health institutions in Jiangsu Province to deliver comprehensive HBV prevention and control services, addressing gaps in diagnosis, referral, treatment, and follow-up management despite progress in HBV prevention in China.
Design and population
A cross-sectional survey was conducted across 13 prefecture-level cities in Jiangsu Province, encompassing 183 medical and health institutions, including 135 hospitals and 48 disease control and prevention centers.
Core methods
The survey assessed the capacity for HBsAg testing, HBV-DNA testing, case diagnosis, referral, antiviral treatment, patient follow-up, and public health prevention and control initiatives implemented by CDCs.
Main results
Among the 183 surveyed institutions, 86.9% reported having the capacity to perform HBsAg testing, while HBV-DNA testing was available in 56.8% of institutions, and follow-up management was implemented in 34.1% of hospitals, mainly among tertiary hospitals.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that while HBV laboratory testing capacity, particularly for HBsAg testing, is strong, HBV treatment and follow-up services remain highly concentrated in tertiary hospitals, indicating limited participation of primary-level institutions in long-term HBV management.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest the need for future efforts to strengthen the HBV prevention and control service chain by unifying clinical diagnostic criteria, enhancing referral mechanisms, expanding grassroots follow-up capacity, and promoting coordinated information sharing between healthcare institutions and CDCs.
Limitations and evidence strength
The survey's reliance on self-reported data may introduce bias, and the high concentration of HBV treatment and follow-up services in tertiary hospitals limits the generalizability of the findings to primary-level institutions.

通过机器学习实现针对慢性乙型肝炎相关肝纤维化的个性化预测

Realizing personalized prediction for the reversal of chronic hepatitis B-associated liver fibrosis through machine learning
作者： Shi-Cai Liu, Zi-Wen Wang, Han Zhang
摘要中文翻译尽管长期抗病毒药物治疗可以逆转慢性乙型肝炎患者的肝纤维化,但个体对治疗的反应差异显著,目前仍缺乏可靠的预测方法。传统的临床工具如肝硬度测量和天门冬氨酸氨基转移酶与血小板比值指数最初设计用于横断面分期,而非纵向预测,其捕捉纤维化逆转动态过程的能力有限。在本观点综述中,我们讨论了机器学习(ML)如何帮助解决这一未满足的需求。我们重点关注了机器学习在三个方面的早期但有希望的结果:利用机器学习进行图像的定量分析、通过多模态模型整合病理和临床数据,以及基于抗病毒药物治疗期间的连续测量动态预测治疗反应。我们认为,机器学习的最大价值并不在于取代现有评估工具,而在于补充它们,特别是在早期识别那些仅通过抗病毒药物治疗难以实现纤维化逆转的患者方面。然而,我们也提醒,目前大多数模型都是基于小规模、单中心的回顾性队列研究开发的,其临床应用需要前瞻性多中心验证、提高模型的可解释性以及遵循标准化的报告框架。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决缺乏可靠方法来预测 慢性乙型肝炎 患者个体 治疗 反应的问题,因为长期 抗病毒药物 治疗可以逆转某些个体的肝纤维化,但不能逆转其他个体的肝纤维化。
设计与对象
本意见综述讨论了机器学习在预测 治疗 对 慢性乙型肝炎 反应中的应用,重点关注 ML 补充现有临床工具的潜力。
核心方法
作者回顾了机器学习在三个领域的应用:定量图像分析、通过多模态模型整合病理和临床数据,以及基于 治疗 治疗期间的连续测量动态预测 治疗 反应。
主要结果
该综述强调了机器学习应用在预测 慢性乙型肝炎 纤维化逆转方面的早期但令人鼓舞的结果,特别是通过临床和病理数据的整合。
解释与创新
作者解释说,机器学习可以增强用于评估 慢性乙型肝炎 中 治疗 响应的现有工具,提供一种新颖的个性化预测方法,而无需取代当前方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,机器学习可以提高预测哪些 慢性乙型肝炎 患者可能实现纤维化逆转的精确度,有助于制定更有针对性的 治疗 策略。
局限与证据强度
证据有限,因为大多数当前模型都是基于小型、单中心回顾性 队列研究,这可能会影响其普遍性并需要进一步的前瞻性验证。

Realizing personalized prediction for the reversal of chronic hepatitis B-associated liver fibrosis through machine learning

Authors: Shi-Cai Liu, Zi-Wen Wang, Han Zhang
Original abstract (English)
Although long-term antiviral therapy can reverse liver fibrosis in a considerable proportion of patients with chronic hepatitis B, treatment response differs markedly among individuals, and reliable methods for predicting who will benefit remain lacking. Conventional clinical tools such as liver stiffness measurement and the aspartate aminotransferase-to-platelet ratio index were originally dewsigned for cross-sectional staging rather than longitudinal prediction, and their ability to capture the dynamic process of fibrosis reversal is limited. In this opinion review, we discuss how machine learning (ML) may help address this unmet need. We focus on three areas where ML has shown early but encouraging results: Quantitative analysis of images using ML, integration of pathological and clinical data through multimodal models, and dynamic prediction of treatment response based on serial measurements during antiviral therapy. In our view, the greatest value of ML lies not in replacing existing assessment tools but in complementing them, particularly through the early identification of patients who are unlikely to achieve fibrosis reversal with antiviral therapy alone. However, we also caution that most current models have been developed from small, single-center retrospective cohorts, and their clinical adoption will require prospective multi-center validation, improved model interpretability, and adherence to standardized reporting frameworks.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the lack of reliable methods for predicting individual treatment response in chronic hepatitis B patients, as long-term antiviral therapy can reverse liver fibrosis in some individuals but not others.
Design and population
This opinion review discusses the application of machine learning in predicting treatment response for chronic hepatitis B, focusing on the potential of ML to complement existing clinical tools.
Core methods
The authors review the use of machine learning in three areas: quantitative image analysis, integration of pathological and clinical data through multimodal models, and dynamic prediction of treatment response based on serial measurements during antiviral therapy.
Main results
The review highlights early but encouraging results from machine learning applications in predicting fibrosis reversal in chronic hepatitis B, particularly through the integration of clinical and pathological data.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that machine learning can enhance existing tools for assessing treatment response in chronic hepatitis B, offering a novel approach to personalized prediction without replacing current methods.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that machine learning could improve the precision of predicting which patients with chronic hepatitis B are likely to achieve fibrosis reversal, aiding in more tailored treatment strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited by the fact that most current models are based on small, single-center retrospective cohorts, which may affect their generalizability and require further prospective validation.

非洲 慢性乙型肝炎 患者的肝脂肪变性:患病率、相关性和临床意义。

Hepatic Steatosis in People With Chronic Hepatitis B in Africa: Prevalence, Correlates, and Clinical Significance.
作者： Freeman W Chabala, Edford Sinkala, Alana T Brennan, Amy Miskimon Goss, Lamin Bojang, Yusupha Samateh, Alexander J Stockdale, Roger Sombie, Arouna Sessouma, Alice Nanelin Guingane
摘要中文翻译为了解决非洲地区代谢性肝病的数据缺口,我们在非洲乙型肝炎合作网络(HEPSANET)中评估了慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)患者是否患有脂肪性肝病(SLD),使用了瞬时弹性成像结合控制衰减参数(CAP)。通过多变量逻辑回归分析,我们确定了与SLD(CAP ≥ 248 dB/m)相关的社会人口学、人体测量学和临床特征,并评估了SLD严重程度是否独立与纤维化相关。我们还使用Cox比例风险模型评估了SLD对死亡率的短期影响。此外,我们评估了B型超声波在SLD诊断中的表现,以CAP为参考标准。在6个国家的1601名参与者(年龄范围12-80岁;39.5%女性;4%患有糖尿病;11%有不健康饮酒行为;中位数体重指数24.4)中,421名(26.3%)患有SLD;219名(13.7%)患有严重脂肪变(CAP ≥ 280 dB/m)。年龄较大、男性性别、超重和肥胖以及HIV阴性状态与SLD相关。在调整年龄、性别、HIV状态、HBV 治疗和国家后,严重脂肪变(而非轻度和中度形式,即248-279 dB/m)与纤维化概率增加8.4%(95%置信区间1.9%-14.9%)相关。在有限的随访时间(中位数2.69年)和较少的死亡病例下,SLD与全因或肝相关死亡率无显著关联。B型超声波对SLD的敏感性较低,无论是整体还是在严重情况下,但特异性为95%。总之,SLD在非洲CHB患者中较为常见,其严重形式与肝纤维化相关。这些数据支持在非洲HBV管理中纳入代谢性共病的筛查措施以及治疗的监测。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
为了解决非洲地区代谢性肝病的数据缺口,我们在非洲乙型肝炎合作网络(HEPSANET)中评估了慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)患者是否患有脂肪性肝病(SLD),使用了瞬时弹性成像结合控制衰减参数(CAP).
设计与对象
通过多变量逻辑回归分析,我们确定了与SLD(CAP ≥ 248 dB/m)相关的社会人口学、人体测量学和临床特征,并评估了SLD严重程度是否独立与纤维化相关.
核心方法
在6个国家的1601名参与者(年龄范围12-80岁;39.5%女性;4%患有糖尿病;11%有不健康饮酒行为;中位数体重指数24.4)中,421名(26.3%)患有SLD;219名(13.7%)患有严重脂肪变(CAP ≥ 280 dB/m).
主要结果
年龄较大、男性性别、超重和肥胖以及HIV阴性状态与SLD相关。在调整年龄、性别、HIV状态、HBV 治疗和国家后,严重脂肪变(而非轻度和中度形式,即248-279 dB/m)与纤维化概率增加8.4%(95%置信区间1.9%-14.9%)相关.
解释与创新
总之,SLD在非洲CHB患者中较为常见,其严重形式与肝纤维化相关。
科研与公卫意义
这些数据支持将代谢合并症筛查作为非洲 HBV 护理和 治疗 的一部分。
局限与证据强度
在有限的随访时间(中位数2.69年)和较少的死亡病例下,SLD与全因或肝相关死亡率无显著关联。

Hepatic Steatosis in People With Chronic Hepatitis B in Africa: Prevalence, Correlates, and Clinical Significance.

Authors: Freeman W Chabala, Edford Sinkala, Alana T Brennan, Amy Miskimon Goss, Lamin Bojang, Yusupha Samateh, Alexander J Stockdale, Roger Sombie, Arouna Sessouma, Alice Nanelin Guingane
Original abstract (English)
To address data gaps around metabolic liver disease in Africa, within the Hepatitis B in Africa Collaborative Network (HEPSANET), we evaluated participants with chronic hepatitis B virus infection (CHB) for steatotic liver disease (SLD) using transient elastography with controlled attenuation parameter (CAP). Using multivariable logistic regression, we identified sociodemographic, anthropometric, and clinical associations with SLD (CAP ≥ 248 dB/m), and evaluated whether SLD severity was independently associated with fibrosis. Short-term impacts of SLD on mortality were also assessed with Cox proportional hazards analysis. We also evaluated the diagnostic performance of B-mode ultrasound for SLD, with CAP as reference. Across 6 countries and 1601 participants (age range, 12-80 years; 39.5% female; 4% with diabetes; 11% unhealthy alcohol use; median body mass index, 24.4), 421 (26.3%) had SLD; 219 (13.7%) had severe steatosis (CAP ≥ 280 dB/m). Older age, male sex, overweight and obesity, and HIV-negative status were associated with SLD. After adjusting for age, sex, HIV status, HBV treatment, and country, severe steatosis, but not mild and moderate forms (i.e., 248-279 dB/m), was associated with a 8.4% (95%, 1.9%-14.9%) increased probability of fibrosis. Over limited follow-up (median 2.69 years) and small number of deaths, SLD was not associated with all-cause or liver-related mortality. B-mode ultrasound had low sensitivity for SLD, overall or when severe, but specificity was 95%. In conclusion, SLD was common in people with CHB in Africa and its severe form was associated with liver fibrosis. These data support screening for metabolic comorbidities as part of HBV care and treatment in Africa.
View research seven-element analysis

慢性乙型肝炎 成人的体力活动和 死亡率 风险以及肝脏相关事件:一项全国范围的 队列研究 研究。

Becoming Physically Active and Risks of Mortality and Liver-Related Events in Adults With Chronic Hepatitis B: A Nationwide Cohort Study.
作者： Byeong Geun Song, Myeongcheol Lee, Juhee Cho, Geum-Youn Gwak, Danbee Kang, Dong Hyun Sinn
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
体力活动 (PA) 具有多种健康益处;然而,在缺乏身体活动的 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 成人中增加 PA 是否与较低的不良临床结果风险相关尚不清楚。
设计与对象
我们纳入了 318,131 名慢性乙型肝炎成人患者,他们在初次健康筛查(检查 1)时身体缺乏活动,并在 4 年内完成了后续筛查(检查 2)。
核心方法
我们使用了全国性的韩国国民健康保险数据库。
主要结果
在中位数7.9年的随访期间(四分位数范围,5.2-9.5),发生了7609例死亡。
解释与创新
MVPA initiation was associated with lower all-cause mortality (hazard ratio [HR], 0.89; 95% CI, 0.84-0.94), a lower risk of liver-related events (subdistribution HR [subHR], 0.94; 95% CI, 0.92-0.97), and, more modestly, of HCC (subHR, 0.95; 95% CI, 0.91-0.99) compared with those remaining inactive.
科研与公卫意义
这些发现提供了大规模的流行病学证据,支持在慢性乙型肝炎患者中进一步评估体力活动推广的必要性。
局限与证据强度
这种关联在老年人、女性、从未吸烟者、非饮酒者和未接受治疗的参与者中更为显著(所有交互作用的p值≤0.03)。

Becoming Physically Active and Risks of Mortality and Liver-Related Events in Adults With Chronic Hepatitis B: A Nationwide Cohort Study.

Authors: Byeong Geun Song, Myeongcheol Lee, Juhee Cho, Geum-Youn Gwak, Danbee Kang, Dong Hyun Sinn
Original abstract (English)
Physical activity (PA) confers several health benefits; however, whether increasing PA among physically inactive adults with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) is associated with lower risks of adverse clinical outcomes remains unclear. We used the nationwide Korean National Health Insurance database. We included 318,131 adults with CHB who were physically inactive at the initial health screening (Exam 1) and completed a subsequent screening within 4 years (Exam 2). Among them, 58,703 initiated moderate-to-vigorous PA (MVPA), defined as vigorous activity ≥ 20 min/day on ≥ 3 days/week or moderate activity ≥ 30 min/day on ≥ 5 days/week. The primary outcome was all-cause mortality, and the secondary outcomes were liver-related events (hepatic decompensation, liver transplantation, hepatocellular carcinoma [HCC]) and HCC. Multivariable Cox and Fine-Grey competing-risk models were adjusted for demographic, clinical, and behavioural factors. During a median 7.9-year follow-up (IQR, 5.2-9.5), 7609 deaths occurred. MVPA initiation was associated with lower all-cause mortality (hazard ratio [HR], 0.89; 95% CI, 0.84-0.94), a lower risk of liver-related events (subdistribution HR [subHR], 0.94; 95% CI, 0.92-0.97), and, more modestly, of HCC (subHR, 0.95; 95% CI, 0.91-0.99) compared with those remaining inactive. The association was more pronounced in older adults, women, never smokers, non-drinkers, and untreated participants (all p for interaction ≤ 0.03). Initiating MVPA among physically inactive adults with CHB was associated with lower risks of mortality and liver-related outcomes. These findings provide large-scale epidemiological evidence supporting further evaluation of PA promotion in physically inactive patients with CHB.
View research seven-element analysis

Physical activity (PA) confers several health benefits; however, whether increasing PA among physically inactive adults with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) is associated with lower risks of adverse clinical outcomes remains unclear.
Design and population

Core methods
We used the nationwide Korean National Health Insurance database.
Main results
During a median 7.9-year follow-up (IQR, 5.2-9.5), 7609 deaths occurred.
Interpretation and novelty
MVPA initiation was associated with lower all-cause mortality (hazard ratio [HR], 0.89; 95% CI, 0.84-0.94), a lower risk of liver-related events (subdistribution HR [subHR], 0.94; 95% CI, 0.92-0.97), and, more modestly, of HCC (subHR, 0.95; 95% CI, 0.91-0.99) compared with those remaining inactive.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings provide large-scale epidemiological evidence supporting further evaluation of PA promotion in physically inactive patients with CHB.
Limitations and evidence strength
The association was more pronounced in older adults, women, never smokers, non-drinkers, and untreated participants (all p for interaction ≤ 0.03).

与乙型肝炎病毒相关失代偿性肝硬化中伴发的轻微肝性脑病的全脑磁化率改变

Whole-Brain Magnetic Susceptibility Alterations in Minimal Hepatic Encephalopathy Associated With Hepatitis B Virus-Related Decompensated Cirrhosis
作者： Xun Ning, Dongcui Wang, Hong Jin, Xuefei Cui, Danlan Hu, Yuxuan Jiang, Xiangze Chen, Wu Xing
摘要中文翻译摘要 轻微肝性脑病(MHE)是肝硬化的一种临床隐匿性并发症,但其全脑磁化率模式仍未被完全阐明。我们研究了31名健康对照(HCs)、26名无MHE的乙型肝炎病毒相关失代偿性肝硬化患者(NMHE)以及13名MHE患者的区域相对磁化率差异。使用定量磁化率映射分析多回波3.0-T梯度回波数据,并对249个区域标签进行非参数成对检验和Benjamini-Hochberg假发现率(FDR)校正。在FDR校正后,HCs与MHE之间有21个标签存在差异,HCs与NMHE之间有21个标签存在差异,而MHE与NMHE之间没有标签存在差异。七个皮层区域在两种患者与对照的比较中均有重叠,并显示出数值上的HC差异。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在阐明与肝硬化相关的隐匿性并发症——轻微肝性脑病(MHE)的全脑磁化率模式,特别是在乙型肝炎病毒相关疾病背景下。
设计与对象
本研究比较了31名健康对照(HCs)、26名无MHE的乙型肝炎病毒相关失代偿性肝硬化患者(NMHE)以及13名MHE患者,以识别区域磁化率差异。
核心方法
使用定量磁化率映射分析多回波3.0-T梯度回波数据,并对249个区域标签进行非参数成对检验和Benjamini-Hochberg假发现率(FDR)校正。
主要结果
在FDR校正后,HCs与MHE之间有21个区域标签存在差异,HCs与NMHE之间有21个区域标签存在差异,而MHE与NMHE之间没有区域标签存在差异。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,MHE与健康对照及非MHE肝硬化患者相比具有不同的磁化率模式,突显了潜在的神经影像学生物标志物用于MHE检测的可能性。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可能有助于开发用于在乙型肝炎病毒相关肝硬化患者中早期检测MHE的非侵入性神经影像学技术。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于摘要分析,证据基于统计比较,而没有详细的临床或生物学机制,因此置信度中等。

Whole-Brain Magnetic Susceptibility Alterations in Minimal Hepatic Encephalopathy Associated With Hepatitis B Virus-Related Decompensated Cirrhosis

Authors: Xun Ning, Dongcui Wang, Hong Jin, Xuefei Cui, Danlan Hu, Yuxuan Jiang, Xiangze Chen, Wu Xing
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Minimal hepatic encephalopathy (MHE) is a clinically covert complication of cirrhosis, but its whole-brain magnetic susceptibility pattern remains incompletely characterized. We investigated regional relative susceptibility in 31 healthy controls (HCs), 26 patients with hepatitis B virus-related decompensated cirrhosis without MHE (NMHE), and 13 patients with MHE. Multi-echo 3.0-T gradient-echo data were reconstructed using quantitative susceptibility mapping, and 249 regional labels were analyzed with nonparametric pairwise tests and Benjamini-Hochberg false discovery rate (FDR) correction. Twenty-one labels differed between HCs and MHE and 21 between HCs and NMHE, whereas no label differed between MHE and NMHE after FDR correction. Seven cortical regions were shared by both patient-versus-control comparisons and showed a numerical HC
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to characterize the whole-brain magnetic susceptibility pattern in minimal hepatic encephalopathy (MHE), a covert complication of cirrhosis, particularly in the context of hepatitis B virus-related disease.
Design and population
The study compared 31 healthy controls (HCs), 26 patients with hepatitis B virus-related decompensated cirrhosis without MHE (NMHE), and 13 patients with MHE to identify regional susceptibility differences.
Core methods
Quantitative susceptibility mapping was used to analyze multi-echo 3.0-T gradient-echo data, with nonparametric pairwise tests and Benjamini-Hochberg false discovery rate (FDR) correction applied to 249 regional labels.
Main results
Twenty-one regional labels showed differences between HCs and MHE, and 21 between HCs and NMHE, but no labels differed between MHE and NMHE after FDR correction.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest distinct susceptibility patterns in MHE compared to healthy controls and non-MHE cirrhosis patients, highlighting potential neuroimaging biomarkers for MHE detection.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results may inform the development of non-invasive neuroimaging techniques for early detection of MHE in patients with hepatitis B virus-related cirrhosis.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an abstract-only analysis, and the evidence is based on statistical comparisons without detailed clinical or biological mechanisms, resulting in moderate confidence.

半定量超声评分揭示慢性乙型肝炎(慢性乙型肝炎)在接受替诺福韦艾拉酚胺治疗期间肝脏结构恢复和免疫调节

Semi-quantitative ultrasound scoring reveals hepatic structural recovery and immune modulation during tenofovir alafenamide therapy in chronic hepatitis B
作者： Wen-Juan Li, Li-Hui Yang, Juan He, Wenjuan Li, Lihui Yang
摘要中文翻译本研究旨在利用半定量超声(US)评分系统评估替诺福韦艾拉酚胺二甲基磺酸盐(TAF)在慢性乙型肝炎(慢性乙型肝炎)患者中的治疗反应,并探讨超声影像变化与免疫功能恢复之间的相关性。方法 入选了100例接受TAF单药治疗(25 mg/天)至少24个月的慢性乙型肝炎患者进行回顾性分析。在基线、12个月和24个月时计算了包含肝脏回声、包膜轮廓、边缘形态、血管清晰度、门静脉直径(PVD)、脾脏厚度(ST)和脾静脉血流速度(SVV)的七参数半定量超声评分。同时测量了血清乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)脱氧核糖核酸(DNA)、丙氨酸氨基转移酶(ALT)、天门冬氨酸氨基转移酶(AST)以及外周T细胞亚群,包括分化簇3阳性(CD3+)、CD4+、CD8+ T细胞、CD4+/CD8+比值和自然杀伤(NK)细胞活性。分析了超声评分与生化和免疫参数之间的相关性。结果 在24个月时,所有患者均达到HBV DNA <20 IU/mL,ALT和AST的正常化率分别为92%和95%。综合超声评分从基线的7.86 ± 1.73显著下降至24个月的3.37 ± 0.95(P < 0.001),其中包膜平滑度和实质均匀性的改善最为显著。CD4+ T细胞比例和CD4+/CD8+比值增加,而CD8+ T细胞比例下降;NK细胞活性从42.7 ± 7.3%上升至52.4 ± 6.2%(P < 0.05)。超声评分与ALT(r = 0.56)和AST(r = 0.47)呈正相关,与CD4+/CD8+比值(r = −0.46)和NK细胞活性(r = −0.38)呈负相关(所有P < 0.01)。结论 这些发现表明,TAF不仅抑制病毒复制并使肝酶正常化,还促进可测量的肝脏结构改善,这体现在半定量超声评分的下降。超声评分与生化和免疫参数之间的强相关性支持其作为非侵入性影像生物标志物用于监测治疗疗效和肝脏修复的潜力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在利用半定量超声(US)评分系统评估替诺福韦艾拉酚胺二甲基磺酸盐(TAF)在慢性乙型肝炎(慢性乙型肝炎)患者中的治疗反应,并探讨超声影像变化与免疫功能恢复之间的相关性。
设计与对象
回顾性纳入 100 名接受 TAF 单药治疗(25 毫克/天)至少 24 个月的慢性乙型肝炎患者。
核心方法
在基线、12 个月和 24 个月时计算七参数半定量 US 评分,包括肝脏回声性、包膜轮廓、边缘形状、血管清晰度、门静脉直径 (PVD)、脾厚度 (ST) 和脾静脉速度 (SVV)。同时,测量血清 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 脱氧核糖核酸 (DNA)、丙氨酸转氨酶 (ALT)、天冬氨酸转氨酶 (AST)、外周 T 细胞亚群,包括分化簇 3 阳性 (CD3 + )、CD4 + 、CD8 + T 细胞、CD4 + / CD8 + 比率和自然杀伤 (NK) 细胞活性。
主要结果
在24个月时,所有患者均达到HBV DNA <20 IU/mL,ALT和AST的正常化率分别为92%和95%。综合超声评分从基线的7.86 ± 1.73显著下降至24个月的3.37 ± 0.95(P < 0.001),其中包膜平滑度和实质均匀性的改善最为显著。
解释与创新
分析了美国评分与生化/免疫参数之间的相关性。US评分与ALT(r = 0.56)和AST(r = 0.47)呈正相关,与CD4 + /CD8 + 比值(r = -0.46)和NK细胞活性(r = -0.38)呈负相关(均P < 0.01)。
科研与公卫意义
超声评分与生化和免疫参数之间的强相关性支持其作为非侵入性影像生物标志物用于监测治疗疗效和肝脏修复的潜力。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性。

Semi-quantitative ultrasound scoring reveals hepatic structural recovery and immune modulation during tenofovir alafenamide therapy in chronic hepatitis B

Authors: Wen-Juan Li, Li-Hui Yang, Juan He, Wenjuan Li, Lihui Yang
Original abstract (English)
Objective This study aimed to evaluate the therapeutic response to tenofovir alafenamide fumarate (TAF) in chronic hepatitis B (CHB) using a semi-quantitative ultrasound (US) scoring system, and to investigate the correlation between sonographic changes and immune function restoration. Methods A total of 100 CHB patients who received TAF monotherapy (25 mg/day) for at least 24 months were retrospectively enrolled. A seven-parameter semi-quantitative US score, encompassing liver echogenicity, capsular contour, margin shape, vessel clarity, portal vein diameter (PVD), spleen thickness (ST), and splenic vein velocity (SVV), was calculated at baseline, 12 months, and 24 months. Concurrently, serum hepatitis B virus (HBV) deoxyribonucleic acid (DNA), alanine aminotransferase (ALT), aspartate aminotransferase (AST), peripheral T-cell subsets, including cluster of differentiation 3-positive (CD3 + ), CD4 + , CD8 + T cells, CD4+/CD8+ ratio, and natural killer (NK) cell activity were measured. Correlations between US scores and biochemical/immune parameters were analyzed. Results At 24 months, all patients achieved HBV DNA <20 IU/mL, with ALT and AST normalization rates of 92% and 95%, respectively. The comprehensive US score decreased significantly from 7.86 ± 1.73 at baseline to 3.37 ± 0.95 at 24 months ( P < 0.001), with the most pronounced improvements observed in capsular smoothness and parenchymal homogeneity. The CD4 + T-cell proportion and CD4 + /CD8 + ratio increased, whereas the CD8 + proportion decreased; NK cell activity rose from 42.7 ± 7.3% to 52.4 ± 6.2% ( P < 0.05). The US score was positively correlated with ALT (r = 0.56) and AST (r = 0.47), and negatively correlated with the CD4 + /CD8 + ratio (r = −0.46) and NK cell activity (r = −0.38) (all P < 0.01). Conclusion These findings indicate that TAF not only suppresses viral replication and normalises liver enzymes but also promotes measurable structural liver improvement, as reflected by declining semi-quantitative US scores. The strong correlations of US scores with biochemical and immune parameters support its utility as a non-invasive imaging biomarker for monitoring treatment efficacy and hepatic repair.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the therapeutic response to tenofovir alafenamide fumarate (TAF) in chronic hepatitis B (CHB) using a semi-quantitative ultrasound (US) scoring system, and to investigate the correlation between sonographic changes and immune function restoration.

The comprehensive US score decreased significantly from 7.86 ± 1.73 at baseline to 3.37 ± 0.95 at 24 months (P < 0.001), with the most pronounced improvements observed in capsular smoothness and parenchymal homogeneity.

The strong correlations of US scores with biochemical and immune parameters support its utility as a non-invasive imaging biomarker for monitoring treatment efficacy and hepatic repair.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations.

美国乙型肝炎病毒疫苗接种与新生儿死亡率的关系

Hepatitis B vaccination and neonatal mortality in the United States
作者： Pallab Kumar Ghosh, Ahmed El Fatmaoui, Jun-Ying Zhao
摘要中文翻译我们利用美国县一级面板数据(2011–2019年)和一个将历史生育率与全国疫苗价格波动相交互的移位共享工具变量,估计了乙型肝炎病毒出生剂量疫苗接种对新生儿死亡率的可能因果效应。在控制县固定效应和历史生育率份额与线性趋势交互后,出生剂量覆盖率每增加一个百分点,可使0–3天新生儿死亡率死亡率降低0.022例/1000活产,相当于平均下降1.04%。该效应主要集中在出生第一天,而在后续婴儿年龄阶段则与零不可区分,这种模式可能与早期生命机制或行政机制有关,但我们并未识别出任何一种机制。该估计是基于线性生育趋势的无权重县平均值,我们同时报告了支持该估计的诊断结果以及对其的质疑诊断结果。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在估计乙型肝炎病毒出生剂量疫苗接种对美国新生儿死亡率的可能因果效应。
设计与对象
本研究使用了2011年至2019年美国县一级面板数据,并结合了一个将历史生育率与全国疫苗价格波动相交互的移位共享工具变量。
核心方法
分析中纳入了县固定效应,并控制了历史生育率份额与线性趋势的交互作用。
主要结果
出生剂量覆盖率每增加一个百分点,可使0–3天新生儿死亡率死亡率降低0.022例/1000活产,相当于平均下降1.04%。
解释与创新
该效应主要集中在出生第一天,而在后续婴儿年龄阶段则与零不可区分,这种模式可能与早期生命机制或行政机制有关,但我们并未识别出任何一种机制。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为乙型肝炎病毒疫苗接种对新生儿死亡率的潜在影响提供了见解,可能有助于制定疫苗的公共卫生策略。
局限与证据强度
该估计是基于线性生育趋势的无权重县平均值,我们同时报告了支持该估计的诊断结果以及对其的质疑诊断结果。

Hepatitis B vaccination and neonatal mortality in the United States

Authors: Pallab Kumar Ghosh, Ahmed El Fatmaoui, Jun-Ying Zhao
Original abstract (English)
We estimate the plausibly causal effect of hepatitis B birth dose vaccination on neonatal mortality using U.S. county-level panel data (2011–2019) and a shift-share instrument that interacts historical fertility rates with national vaccine price variation. With county fixed effects and a control for the historical fertility share interacted with a linear trend, a one percentage point increase in birth dose coverage reduces 0–3 day mortality by 0.022 deaths per 1,000 live births, representing a 1.04% decline at the mean. The effect is concentrated in the first day of life and is indistinguishable from zero at later infant ages, a profile consistent either with an early life mechanism or with an administrative one, and we identify neither. The estimate is an unweighted county average conditional on a linear fertility trend, and we report the diagnostics that qualify it alongside those that support it.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to estimate the plausibly causal effect of hepatitis B birth dose vaccination on neonatal mortality in the United States.
Design and population
The research used county-level panel data from 2011 to 2019 and a shift-share instrument that interacts historical fertility rates with national vaccine price variation.
Core methods
The analysis incorporated county fixed effects and controlled for the historical fertility share interacted with a linear trend.
Main results
A one percentage point increase in birth dose coverage reduces 0–3 day mortality by 0.022 deaths per 1,000 live births, representing a 1.04% decline at the mean.
Interpretation and novelty
The effect is concentrated in the first day of life and is indistinguishable from zero at later infant ages, a profile consistent either with an early life mechanism or with an administrative one, and we identify neither.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide insights into the potential impact of hepatitis B vaccination on neonatal mortality, which could inform public health strategies for vaccine implementation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The estimate is an unweighted county average conditional on a linear fertility trend, and we report the diagnostics that qualify it alongside those that support it.

接受核苷(酸)类似物治疗的慢性乙型肝炎患者中肠道微生物群组成与HBeAg和HBsAg状态的探索性关联

Exploratory associations of gut microbiota composition with HBeAg and HBsAg status in nucleos(t)ide analog-treated chronic hepatitis B
作者： Si-Si Yang, Si-Qi Sun, Ji-Yang Chen, Ya-Ni Zhou, Fang-Ping Jia, Yi-Ran Xie, Ying-Feng Lu, Qin Ni, Jian-Hua Hu, Mei-Fang Yang
摘要中文翻译摘要 HBeAg和HBsAg反映了病毒复制和宿主免疫状态,是治疗应答的重要决定因素。肠道微生物群作为肠道-肝脏轴的关键组成部分,可能影响免疫调节和抗病毒药物结果。本研究分析了接受核苷(酸)类似物(NA)治疗的慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)患者中不同HBeAg和HBsAg状态下的肠道微生物群特征。对29名健康对照(HC)和101名无肝硬化或肝细胞癌的NA治疗CHB患者进行了粪便宏基因组 shotgun 测序,其中包括30名HBeAg阳性(EP)和71名HBeAg阴性(EN)个体。EN组进一步根据血清HBsAg水平分为优势亚组(<1,500 IU/mL,n = 57)和非优势亚组(≥1,500 IU/mL,n = 14)。EP、EN和HC组的α多样性和β多样性均无显著差异。种群水平分析发现,与Bacteroidales和Firmicutes相关的厌氧共生菌存在组间差异,包括Bacteroides uniformis、Blautia obeum、Eubacterium ventriosum、Anaerobutyricum和Parabacteroides timonensis;然而,在所有检测到的物种中,这些差异在全局FDR校正后均不显著。在EN组中,选定的菌群在下采样、留一法和CLR基础上的敏感性分析中显示出方向一致的关联,但这些亚组发现同样在FDR校正后不显著。接受NA治疗的CHB患者与肠道微生物群的探索性分类差异相关,但未观察到微生物多样性的明显损失。观察到的与HBeAg状态和HBsAg负担的关联具有假设生成性,需要在更大规模的纵向队列研究研究中进行验证,该研究应纳入绝对微生物定量、靶向代谢组学、宿主免疫表型以及更全面的饮食、人体测量和其他相关临床协变量。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨接受核苷(酸)类似物(NA)治疗的慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)患者中肠道微生物群组成与HBeAg和HBsAg状态之间的关联,因为这些标志物反映了病毒复制和宿主免疫状态,并且对治疗应答至关重要。
设计与对象
本研究分析了29名健康对照(HC)和101名无肝硬化或肝细胞癌的NA治疗CHB患者的肠道微生物群特征,其中包括30名HBeAg阳性(EP)和71名HBeAg阴性(EN)个体。EN组进一步根据血清HBsAg水平分为优势亚组(<1,500 IU/mL,n = 57)和非优势亚组(≥1,500 IU/mL,n = 14)。
核心方法
使用粪便宏基因组 shotgun 测序来分析接受核苷(酸)类似物(NA)治疗的CHB患者中不同HBeAg和HBsAg状态下的肠道微生物群特征。
主要结果
EP、EN和HC组的α多样性和β多样性均无显著差异。种群水平分析发现,与Bacteroidales和Firmicutes相关的厌氧共生菌存在组间差异,但这些差异在全局FDR校正后均不显著。在EN组中,选定的菌群在下采样、留一法和CLR基础上的敏感性分析中显示出方向一致的关联,但这些发现同样在FDR校正后不显著。
解释与创新
观察到的与HBeAg状态和HBsAg负担的关联具有假设生成性,提示肠道微生物群可能与CHB患者的病毒结果存在潜在联系,但需要进一步验证。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为未来研究肠道微生物群在CHB进展和治疗应答中的作用提供信息,可能有助于开发基于微生物群的生物标志物或干预措施。
局限与证据强度
观察到的关联在FDR校正后均不具有统计学意义,且本研究缺乏纵向数据、绝对微生物定量以及全面的临床协变量,限制了证据的强度。

Exploratory associations of gut microbiota composition with HBeAg and HBsAg status in nucleos(t)ide analog-treated chronic hepatitis B

Authors: Si-Si Yang, Si-Qi Sun, Ji-Yang Chen, Ya-Ni Zhou, Fang-Ping Jia, Yi-Ran Xie, Ying-Feng Lu, Qin Ni, Jian-Hua Hu, Mei-Fang Yang
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT HBeAg and HBsAg reflect viral replication and host immune status and are important determinants of treatment responsiveness in chronic hepatitis B (CHB). The gut microbiota, a key component of the gut-liver axis, may influence immune regulation and antiviral outcomes. This study characterized gut microbiota profiles across different HBeAg and HBsAg statuses in CHB patients receiving nucleos(t)ide analog (NA) therapy. Fecal shotgun metagenomic sequencing was performed in 29 healthy controls (HC) and 101 NA-treated CHB patients without cirrhosis or hepatocellular carcinoma, including 30 HBeAg-positive (EP) and 71 HBeAg-negative (EN) individuals. The EN group was further stratified by serum HBsAg levels into an advantageous subgroup (<1,500 IU/mL, n = 57) and a non-advantageous subgroup (≥1,500 IU/mL, n = 14). Alpha and beta diversity did not differ significantly among EP, EN, and HC groups. Species-level analyses identified nominal group-related differences in several Bacteroidales- and Firmicutes-associated anaerobic commensals, including Bacteroides uniformis, Blautia obeum, Eubacterium ventriosum, Anaerobutyricum, and Parabacteroides timonensis; however, none remained significant after global FDR correction across all detected species. Within the EN group, selected taxa showed directionally consistent associations with HBsAg strata in downsampling, leave-one-out, and CLR-based sensitivity analyses, but these subgroup findings also did not remain significant after FDR correction. NA-treated CHB was associated with exploratory taxonomic differences without detectable loss of microbial diversity. The observed associations with HBeAg status and HBsAg burden were hypothesis-generating and require validation in larger longitudinal cohorts incorporating absolute microbial quantification, targeted metabolomics, host immune profiling, and more complete dietary, anthropometric, and other relevant clinical covariates.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to explore the associations between gut microbiota composition and HBeAg and HBsAg status in patients with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) receiving nucleos(t)ide analog (NA) therapy, as these markers reflect viral replication and host immune status and are critical for treatment responsiveness.
Design and population
The study analyzed gut microbiota profiles in 29 healthy controls (HC) and 101 NA-treated CHB patients without cirrhosis or hepatocellular carcinoma, including 30 HBeAg-positive (EP) and 71 HBeAg-negative (EN) individuals. The EN group was further stratified by serum HBsAg levels into an advantageous subgroup (<1,500 IU/mL, n = 57) and a non-advantageous subgroup (≥1,500 IU/mL, n = 14).
Core methods
Fecal shotgun metagenomic sequencing was used to characterize gut microbiota profiles across different HBeAg and HBsAg statuses in CHB patients receiving nucleos(t)ide analog (NA) therapy.
Main results
Alpha and beta diversity did not differ significantly among EP, EN, and HC groups. Species-level analyses identified nominal group-related differences in several Bacteroidales- and Firmicutes-associated anaerobic commensals, but none remained significant after global FDR correction. Within the EN group, selected taxa showed directionally consistent associations with HBsAg strata in various sensitivity analyses, but these findings also did not remain significant after FDR correction.
Interpretation and novelty
The observed associations with HBeAg status and HBsAg burden were hypothesis-generating and suggest potential links between gut microbiota and viral outcomes in CHB patients, though further validation is needed.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings may inform future research on the role of gut microbiota in CHB progression and treatment response, potentially guiding the development of microbiota-based biomarkers or interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The associations observed were not statistically significant after FDR correction, and the study lacked longitudinal data, absolute microbial quantification, and comprehensive clinical covariates, limiting the strength of the evidence.

一种双重组策略用于通过ELISA定量乙型肝炎病毒基因型B或C患者的PreS1抗体

A dual recombinant strategy to quantify PreS1 antibody in hepatitis B patients with genotype B or C virus by ELISA
作者： Yu-Min Li, Xiao-Yu Wen, Jing Zhan, Ting-Ting Cao, Xiumei Chi, Huan-Yi Cheng, Qing-Rui Huang, Wen-Yi Zhang, Jun-Qi Niu, Huan Du
摘要中文翻译患者体内抗PreS1抗体水平升高与更快的乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)清除相关,因为PreS1对病毒进入至关重要。然而,由于HBV基因型的差异,准确量化PreS1抗体受到阻碍。本文开发了一种针对中国主要为基因型B和C的乙型肝炎病毒患者的定量PreS1抗体检测方法。首先,为应对基因型多样性,我们构建了一种结合基因型B和C PreS1的双重组蛋白。其次,通过筛选和克隆,开发了一种对B和C型PreS1具有高且可比亲和力的小鼠-人嵌合抗体,作为定量的标准。使用这些组分优化的ELISA试剂盒表现出良好的特异性、准确度、可重复性和稳定性,线性范围为10–100 ng/mL。该方法将中国患者的覆盖范围扩展至超过95%,提供了一种有价值的诊断工具。本研究展示了一种开发适用于其他多基因型病毒疾病的广泛覆盖检测方法的概念验证模型。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
患者体内抗PreS1抗体水平升高与更快的乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)清除相关,因为PreS1对病毒进入至关重要。
设计与对象
本文开发了一种针对中国主要为乙型肝炎病毒基因型B和C的患者的定量PreS1抗体检测方法。
核心方法
使用这些组分优化的ELISA试剂盒表现出良好的特异性、准确度、可重复性和稳定性,线性范围为10–100 ng/mL。
主要结果
该方法将中国患者的覆盖范围扩展至超过95%,提供了一种有价值的诊断工具。
解释与创新
本研究展示了一种开发适用于其他多基因型病毒疾病的广泛覆盖检测方法的概念验证模型。
科研与公卫意义
该方法为中国乙型肝炎病毒患者提供了一种有价值的诊断工具,增强了对病毒清除和疾病进展的监测能力。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据未报告研究设计的局限性或证据强度评估。

A dual recombinant strategy to quantify PreS1 antibody in hepatitis B patients with genotype B or C virus by ELISA

Authors: Yu-Min Li, Xiao-Yu Wen, Jing Zhan, Ting-Ting Cao, Xiumei Chi, Huan-Yi Cheng, Qing-Rui Huang, Wen-Yi Zhang, Jun-Qi Niu, Huan Du
Original abstract (English)
Elevated levels of anti-preS1 antibodies in patients correlate with faster hepatitis B virus (HBV) clearance, since preS1 is vital for viral entry. However, accurate quantification of preS1 antibodies is hindered by the variation of HBV genotypes. Herein, a quantitative preS1 antibody assay was developed for Chinese patients with predominant HBV genotypes B and C. First, to address genotype diversity, we constructed a dual recombinant protein combining genotype B and C preS1. Second, a mouse-human chimeric antibody with high and comparable affinity for both B and C preS1 is developed as the standard for quantification by screening and cloning. An optimized ELISA kit using these components demonstrated good specificity, accuracy, reproducibility and stability, with a linear range of 10–100 ng/mL. This approach extends the patient coverage in China to over 95%, providing a valuable diagnostic tool. This work demonstrates a proof-of-concept model for developing broad-coverage assays for other multi-genotype viral diseases.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Elevated levels of anti-preS1 antibodies in patients correlate with faster hepatitis B virus (HBV) clearance, since preS1 is vital for viral entry.
Design and population
Herein, a quantitative preS1 antibody assay was developed for Chinese patients with predominant HBV genotypes B and C.
Core methods
An optimized ELISA kit using these components demonstrated good specificity, accuracy, reproducibility and stability, with a linear range of 10–100 ng/mL.
Main results
This approach extends the patient coverage in China to over 95%, providing a valuable diagnostic tool.
Interpretation and novelty
This work demonstrates a proof-of-concept model for developing broad-coverage assays for other multi-genotype viral diseases.
Scientific and public-health significance
This approach provides a valuable diagnostic tool for HBV patients in China, enhancing the ability to monitor immune responses and disease progression.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations of the study design or evidence strength assessment.
