血清乙型肝炎病毒标志物反映共价闭合环状DNA和跨慢性乙型肝炎各阶段的转录活性

Serum hepatitis B virus markers reflect intrahepatic covalently closed circular DNA and transcriptional activity across chronic hepatitis B phases
作者： Hye Seon Kim, Jeong Won Jang, Jae Mo Han, Hee Sun Cho, Kwon Yong Tak, Soon Kyu Lee, Pil Soo Sung, Jung Hyun Kwon, Si Hyun Bae, Hyun Woong Lee
摘要中文翻译背景 共价闭合环状DNA(共价闭合环状DNA)通过其转录活性维持乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)的持续感染。尽管共价闭合环状DNA和前基因组RNA(pgRNA)是HBV复制的核心,但反映共价闭合环状DNA活性的血清标志物在慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)各阶段仍不明确。目的 为了识别反映肝内共价闭合环状DNA及其活性的血清标志物,我们分析了一组涵盖所有免疫学阶段的CHB患者队列。方法 对406名未接受治疗治疗的CHB患者按疾病阶段进行分析,包括56例乙肝e抗原(HBeAg)阳性慢性HBV感染(EPCI)、153例HBeAg阳性慢性乙型肝炎(EPCH)、83例HBeAg阴性慢性HBV感染(ENCI)和114例HBeAg阴性慢性乙型肝炎(ENCH)。使用商业检测方法量化血清标志物(乙肝表面抗原(HBsAg)、乙肝核心相关抗原(HBcrAg)和HBV RNA)以及肝内标志物(共价闭合环状DNA和pgRNA)。在肝内共价闭合环状DNA水平相似的患者中评估转录活性。结果 所有血清和肝内标志物均与年龄呈负相关,且HBeAg阳性患者的水平高于HBeAg阴性患者。血清和肝内标志物之间的相关性在HBeAg阳性患者中更为显著。血清标志物与pgRNA的相关性比与共价闭合环状DNA的相关性更紧密。血清HBcrAg和HBV RNA比HBsAg更能反映共价闭合环状DNA的转录活性。在5/34具有相似共价闭合环状DNA水平的120例患者中,肝内共价闭合环状DNA的转录活性(pgRNA/共价闭合环状DNA)在CHB各阶段有所不同:EPCI阶段最高,ENCI阶段最低,EPCH阶段中等,ENCH阶段再次升高。HBcrAg和HBV RNA在EPCH和ENCH阶段与共价闭合环状DNA的转录活性关联更强。值得注意的是,在ENCI阶段,没有血清标志物能可靠反映pgRNA或转录活性。结论 HBcrAg和HBV RNA是共价闭合环状DNA转录活性的阶段依赖性替代标志物。ENCI阶段缺乏可靠的标志物突显了其病毒学异质性。这些发现支持针对不同疾病阶段进行阶段特异性监测和治疗决策。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在识别反映慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)各阶段肝内共价闭合环状DNA及其转录活性的血清标志物。背景强调了更好地理解与共价闭合环状DNA相关的血清标志物的重要性,因为共价闭合环状DNA是HBV持续感染的核心。
设计与对象
本研究分析了406名未接受治疗治疗的CHB患者的样本,按免疫学阶段分类:56例HBeAg阳性慢性HBV感染(EPCI)、153例HBeAg阳性慢性乙型肝炎(EPCH)、83例HBeAg阴性慢性HBV感染(ENCI)和114例HBeAg阴性慢性乙型肝炎(ENCH)。
核心方法
使用商业检测方法量化血清标志物(HBsAg、HBcrAg和HBV RNA)以及肝内标志物(共价闭合环状DNA和pgRNA)。在肝内共价闭合环状DNA水平相似的患者中评估转录活性。
主要结果
所有血清和肝内标志物均与年龄呈负相关,且HBeAg阳性患者的水平高于HBeAg阴性患者。在EPCH和ENCH阶段,血清HBcrAg和HBV RNA与共价闭合环状DNA的转录活性关联比HBsAg更强。值得注意的是,在ENCI阶段,没有血清标志物能可靠反映pgRNA或转录活性。
解释与创新
作者得出结论,HBcrAg和HBV RNA是共价闭合环状DNA转录活性的阶段依赖性替代标志物,而ENCI阶段缺乏可靠标志物突显了其病毒学异质性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持在不同疾病阶段进行阶段特异性监测和治疗决策,强调了选择适当血清标志物的重要性。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于依赖商业检测方法,并且仅针对单一队列研究进行分析。证据强度中等,因为结果基于相关性而非直接因果关系。

Serum hepatitis B virus markers reflect intrahepatic covalently closed circular DNA and transcriptional activity across chronic hepatitis B phases

Authors: Hye Seon Kim, Jeong Won Jang, Jae Mo Han, Hee Sun Cho, Kwon Yong Tak, Soon Kyu Lee, Pil Soo Sung, Jung Hyun Kwon, Si Hyun Bae, Hyun Woong Lee
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND Covalently closed circular DNA (cccDNA) maintains hepatitis B virus (HBV) persistence through its transcriptional activity.While cccDNA and pregenomic RNA (pgRNA) are central to HBV replication, serum markers reflecting active cccDNA remain poorly defined across the phases of chronic hepatitis B (CHB).AIM To identify serum markers reflecting intrahepatic cccDNA and its activity, we analyzed a large CHB cohort encompassing all immunological phases.METHODS Samples from 406 treatment-naïve CHB patients were analyzed by disease phase, including 56 hepatitis B e antigen (HBeAg)-positive chronic HBV infection (EPCI), 153 HBeAg-positive-chronic hepatitis B (EPCH), 83 HBeAg-negative chronic HBV infection (ENCI), and 114 HBeAg-negative chronic hepatitis B (ENCH).Serum markers [hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg), hepatitis B core-related antigen (HBcrAg), and HBV RNA]and intrahepatic markers (cccDNA and pgRNA) were quantified with commercial assays.Transcriptional activity was evaluated in patients with comparable intrahepatic cccDNA levels. RESULTSAll serum and intrahepatic markers were inversely associated with age and were higher in HBeAg-positive than in HBeAg-negative patients.Correlations among serum and intrahepatic markers appeared higher in HBeAg-positive patients.Serum markers correlated more closely with pgRNA than with cccDNA.Serum HBcrAg and HBV RNA better reflected intrahepatic markers than HBsAg.Among 120 patients with comparable 5 / 34 cccDNA levels, intrahepatic cccDNA transcriptional activity (pgRNA/cccDNA) varied across CHB phases: Highest in EPCI, lowest in ENCI, moderate in EPCH, and reelevated in ENCH.HBcrAg and HBV RNA showed stronger associations with cccDNA transcriptional activity than HBsAg across the EPCH and ENCH phases.Notably, no serum marker reliably reflected pgRNA or transcriptional activity in the ENCI phase. CONCLUSIONHBcrAg and HBV RNA are phase-dependent surrogates of transcriptionally active cccDNA.The lack of reliable markers in ENCI highlights its virologic heterogeneity.These findings support phase-specific monitoring and treatment decisions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to identify serum markers reflecting intrahepatic cccDNA and its transcriptional activity across the phases of chronic hepatitis B (CHB). The background highlights the need for better understanding of serum markers that correlate with cccDNA, which is central to HBV persistence.
Design and population
The study analyzed samples from 406 treatment-naïve CHB patients, categorized by immunological phases: 56 HBeAg-positive chronic HBV infection (EPCI), 153 HBeAg-positive chronic hepatitis B (EPCH), 83 HBeAg-negative chronic HBV infection (ENCI), and 114 HBeAg-negative chronic hepatitis B (ENCH).
Core methods
Serum markers [HBsAg, HBcrAg, and HBV RNA] and intrahepatic markers (cccDNA and pgRNA) were quantified using commercial assays. Transcriptional activity was evaluated in patients with comparable intrahepatic cccDNA levels.
Main results
All serum and intrahepatic markers were inversely associated with age and higher in HBeAg-positive than in HBeAg-negative patients. Serum HBcrAg and HBV RNA showed stronger associations with cccDNA transcriptional activity than HBsAg across the EPCH and ENCH phases. Notably, no serum marker reliably reflected pgRNA or transcriptional activity in the ENCI phase.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that HBcrAg and HBV RNA are phase-dependent surrogates of transcriptionally active cccDNA, while the lack of reliable markers in ENCI highlights its virologic heterogeneity.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support the use of phase-specific monitoring and treatment decisions for chronic hepatitis B, emphasizing the importance of selecting appropriate serum markers for different disease phases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on commercial assays and the focus on a single cohort. The evidence is moderate, as the results are based on correlations rather than direct causation.

美国住院孕妇中乙型肝炎病毒和丁型肝炎病毒感染的结局:一项全国性研究(1998–2022)

Outcomes of hepatitis B and hepatitis delta infection in hospitalized pregnant women in the United States: a nationwide study, 1998–2022
作者： Paul Wasuwanich, Tony Soo-Tung Wen, Wikrom W. Karnsakul
摘要中文翻译丁型肝炎病毒(HDV)是一种严重的病毒性肝炎,仅发生在乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染者中。在孕妇人群中,HDV的研究非常有限,且目前尚无相关管理指南。本研究旨在描述并比较同时感染HBV/HDV的孕妇与仅感染HBV的孕妇的临床结局。利用美国全国住院样本数据库,我们提取了1998年至2022年间患有HBV和HDV的孕妇数据,使用的是ICD-9和ICD-10编码。收集了包括共感染、合并疾病、妊娠并发症、胎儿结局和死亡率在内的流行病学和临床数据。分析采用卡方检验和逻辑回归分析。我们确定了390例HDV感染的妊娠和127,332例仅HBV感染的妊娠。在HDV感染的孕妇中,中位年龄为30岁,主要人种/种族为亚洲或太平洋岛屿居民,占27.4%。HDV组中没有产妇死亡病例。HDV感染孕妇的肝炎C病毒感染率(12.1%)高于仅HBV感染孕妇(318例中81.5%已知分娩)。HDV感染孕妇的死产率显著高于仅HBV感染孕妇(2.8% vs. 0.5%),而早产和过期产率相似(p > 0.05)。HDV在妊娠中仍较为罕见,但可能被低估。同时感染HBV/HDV的妊娠可能比仅HBV感染的妊娠具有更高的不良胎儿结局风险,尤其是死产。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在解决对孕妇中丁型肝炎病毒(HDV)管理缺乏指导的问题,因为HDV是一种仅发生在乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染者中的严重病毒性肝炎。
设计与对象
本研究是一项基于美国全国住院样本数据库的全国性回顾性队列研究分析,涵盖了1998年至2022年间患有HBV和HDV的孕妇住院数据。
核心方法
研究人员使用卡方检验和逻辑回归分析,比较同时感染HBV/HDV的孕妇与仅感染HBV的孕妇的流行病学和临床数据。
主要结果
本研究确定了390例HDV感染的妊娠和127,332例仅HBV感染的妊娠。HDV感染孕妇的死产率显著高于仅HBV感染孕妇(2.8% vs. 0.5%),而早产和过期产率相似(p > 0.05)。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,同时感染HBV/HDV可能与比单独HBV感染更高的不良胎儿结局风险相关,尤其是死产。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果突显了在孕妇中提高对HDV的认识和管理的必要性,以降低死产和其他不良结局的风险。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于依赖行政数据,这些数据可能缺乏详细的临床信息。由于研究是观察性的,其证据强度中等。

Outcomes of hepatitis B and hepatitis delta infection in hospitalized pregnant women in the United States: a nationwide study, 1998–2022

Authors: Paul Wasuwanich, Tony Soo-Tung Wen, Wikrom W. Karnsakul
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis delta (HDV) is a severe viral hepatitis disease that occurs only in individuals with hepatitis B (HBV). In the pregnant population, HDV has been scarcely studied, and no guidance exists for its management. We aim to describe and compare a cohort of pregnant women with concurrent HBV/HDV infection to a cohort of pregnant women with only HBV. Utilizing the National Inpatient Sample, a database of hospitalizations throughout the United States, we extracted data from 1998 to 2022 on pregnant women with HBV and HDV using ICD-9 and ICD-10 codes. Demographics and clinical data including co-infection, comorbid condition, pregnancy complications, fetal outcomes, and mortality were collected. Analysis was performed using chi-square and logistic regression. We identified 390 pregnancies with HDV and 127,332 with HBV only. In the HDV cohort, median age was 30 years, and the predominant ethnicity/race was Asian or Pacific Islander at 27.4%. There were no cases of maternal deaths in the HDV group. Rates of hepatitis C (12.1% vs. Of those with HDV, 318 (81.5%) had known delivery. Rates of stillbirth were significantly higher in the HDV cohort (2.8% vs. Rates of preterm and post-term birth were similar ( p > 0.05). HDV remains uncommon in pregnancy but is likely under-recognized. Pregnancies affected by concurrent HBV/HDV infection may carry a higher risk of adverse fetal outcomes, particularly stillbirth, compared to HBV alone.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to address the lack of guidance on managing hepatitis delta (HDV) in pregnant women, as HDV is a severe viral hepatitis disease that occurs only in individuals with hepatitis B (HBV).
Design and population
The study was a nationwide retrospective cohort analysis using the National Inpatient Sample database, covering hospitalizations of pregnant women with HBV and HDV from 1998 to 2022.
Core methods
The researchers used chi-square and logistic regression analyses to compare demographic and clinical data between pregnant women with concurrent HBV/HDV infection and those with HBV alone.
Main results
The study identified 390 pregnancies with HDV and 127,332 with HBV only. Stillbirth rates were significantly higher in the HDV cohort (2.8% vs. 0.5%), while rates of preterm and post-term birth were similar (p > 0.05).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that concurrent HBV/HDV infection may be associated with an increased risk of adverse fetal outcomes, particularly stillbirth, compared to HBV alone.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results highlight the need for improved recognition and management of HDV in pregnant women to reduce the risk of stillbirth and other adverse outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on administrative data, which may lack detailed clinical information. The evidence is moderate due to the observational nature of the study.

Lahore,Pakistan男性献血者中乙型肝炎表面抗原和丙型肝炎抗体血清流行率:一项在四个血库进行的横断面研究

Hepatitis B Surface Antigen and Hepatitis C Antibody Seroprevalence Among Male Blood Donors in Lahore, Pakistan: A Cross-Sectional Study at Four Blood Banks
作者： Muskan Naseer, Farzand Ali, Malaika Jameel Butt, Amna Tariq, Muhammad Salman
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估Lahore,Pakistan男性献血者中乙型肝炎表面抗原(HBsAg)和抗HCV抗体的血清流行率,以应对这些病毒对血液安全造成的挑战。
设计与对象
本研究在Lahore,Pakistan的四个血库中进行,涉及600名男性献血者,每家机构各招募150名。
核心方法
血清样本使用免疫层析快速检测法进行筛查,血型关联性通过卡方检验进行评估,年龄分布则采用Mann–Whitney U检验进行比较。
主要结果
HBsAg seroprevalence was 4.8% (29/600; 95% CI, 3.4–6.9%), and anti-HCV seroprevalence was 4.5% (27/600; 95% CI, 3.1–6.5%). Neither HBsAg nor anti-HCV reactivity was significantly associated with ABO/Rh blood group or age distribution.
解释与创新
研究结果表明,男性献血者中HBsAg和抗HCV的血清流行率相对较低,但强调了可靠筛查和确认检测的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果突显了在资源有限地区持续进行献血者筛查和确认检测以确保血液安全的必要性。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于筛查标记物的血清流行率和快速检测,可能无法确认活动性感染。由于研究设计为横断面且聚焦于特定献血者群体,证据强度中等。

Hepatitis B Surface Antigen and Hepatitis C Antibody Seroprevalence Among Male Blood Donors in Lahore, Pakistan: A Cross-Sectional Study at Four Blood Banks

Authors: Muskan Naseer, Farzand Ali, Malaika Jameel Butt, Amna Tariq, Muhammad Salman
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis C virus (HCV) pose important challenges to blood safety, highlighting the need for continued surveillance among blood donors. This cross-sectional study assessed the seroprevalence of hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) and anti-HCV antibodies among male blood donors at four blood banks in Lahore, Pakistan, and examined their associations with age and ABO/Rh blood group. A total of 600 donors were included, with 150 recruited from each of General Hospital Lahore, Services Hospital Lahore, Noor Thalassaemia Centre, and Zaib Ali Foundation. Serum samples were screened using immunochromatographic rapid tests. Seroprevalence estimates were calculated with 95% Wilson confidence intervals (CIs). Associations with blood group were assessed using chi-square tests, and age distributions were compared using the Mann–Whitney U test. Statistical significance was defined as a two-sided p < 0.05. HBsAg seroprevalence was 4.8% (29/600; 95% CI, 3.4–6.9%), and anti-HCV seroprevalence was 4.5% (27/600; 95% CI, 3.1–6.5%). B-positive was the most frequent blood group (33.0%). Neither HBsAg nor anti-HCV reactivity was significantly associated with ABO/Rh blood group (χ2 = 10.17, p = 0.179 and χ2 = 4.16, p = 0.762, respectively). Age distributions did not differ significantly between reactive and nonreactive donors for either marker (p = 0.210 and p = 0.922, respectively). No significant association was observed between HBsAg and anti-HCV reactivity (p = 0.858). Approximately one in twenty donors screened reactive for each marker, underscoring the importance of reliable donor screening, appropriate confirmatory testing, donor counselling and referral. These findings represent screening-marker seroprevalence rather than confirmed active infection and should be interpreted within the limitations of rapid testing and the sampled male donor population.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to assess the seroprevalence of hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) and anti-HCV antibodies among male blood donors in Lahore, Pakistan, to address the challenges these viruses pose to blood safety.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted at four blood banks in Lahore, Pakistan, involving 600 male blood donors, with 150 recruited from each institution.
Core methods
Serum samples were screened using immunochromatographic rapid tests, and associations with blood group were assessed using chi-square tests, while age distributions were compared using the Mann–Whitney U test.
Main results
HBsAg seroprevalence was 4.8% (29/600; 95% CI, 3.4–6.9%), and anti-HCV seroprevalence was 4.5% (27/600; 95% CI, 3.1–6.5%). Neither HBsAg nor anti-HCV reactivity was significantly associated with ABO/Rh blood group or age distribution.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings indicate that the seroprevalence of HBsAg and anti-HCV among male blood donors is relatively low, but they emphasize the importance of reliable screening and confirmatory testing.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results highlight the need for continued donor screening and confirmatory testing to ensure blood safety, particularly in regions with limited resources.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to screening-marker seroprevalence and rapid testing, which may not confirm active infection. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the cross-sectional design and the focus on a specific donor population.

基于干扰素的乙型肝炎病毒功能性治愈治疗降低低HBsAg慢性乙型肝炎患者肝细胞癌风险:一项倾向匹配研究及动态预测模型

Interferon-based HBV function cure therapy reduces hepatocellular carcinoma risk in low-HBsAg chronic hepatitis B patients: a propensity-matched cohort study and dynamic prediction model
作者： Yu-Rong Gu, Pei-Pei Wang, Qin-Yao Xu, Ting Liu, Hua-Ping Xie, Bao-Er Wu, Yusheng Jie, Xiang-Yong Li, 谢华萍
摘要中文翻译背景:通过HBsAg丢失实现功能性治愈是慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)治疗的关键目标,但其对肝细胞癌(HCC)风险的影响尚未完全阐明,尤其是在通过干扰素(IFN)为基础的治疗实现时。我们旨在评估IFN为基础的治疗在低HBsAg CHB患者中与HCC风险降低的相关性,并为这一人群开发一个经过验证的预测模型。方法:我们对3,367名HBsAg <1,500 IU/mL的CHB患者进行了回顾性研究,其中包括1,780名接受IFN为基础的治疗和1,587名接受核苷(酸)类似物(NAs)单药治疗的患者。使用倾向评分匹配(PSM)来平衡基线特征。采用Cox回归和时间依赖ROC分析来开发HCC的动态列线图预测模型。结果:与NAs单药治疗相比,IFN为基础的治疗与显著更低的HCC发生率相关(1.2% vs. 4.9%,p<0.001),在PSM后,风险比为3.03(95% CI:1.82–5.06)。保护效应在肝硬化患者中最为显著(绝对风险降低8.8%,NNT = 11)。一个结合治疗方式、AFP、肝硬化、年龄及其他变量的新列线图模型显示出出色的预测准确性(5年AUC:推导集为0.888,验证集为0.956),优于现有的HCC预测模型。结论:IFN为基础的治疗显著降低低HBsAg CHB患者的HCC风险,尤其是肝硬化患者。我们开发并验证了一个动态预测模型,可能有助于功能性治愈患者进行个性化的治疗和监测决策。科学及公共卫生意义:这些发现可能为功能性治愈患者提供个性化的治疗和监测决策,从而改善HBV相关肝硬化的患者预后。局限性及证据强度:该研究为回顾性研究,依赖于倾向评分匹配,可能无法完全消除混杂因素。由于研究的观察性质和潜在偏倚,证据强度中等。

问题与背景
通过HBsAg丢失实现功能性治愈是慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)治疗的关键目标,但其对肝细胞癌(HCC)风险的影响仍未完全阐明,尤其是在通过干扰素(IFN)为基础的治疗方案实现时。

科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为功能性治愈患者提供个性化的监测和治疗决策,可能改善与乙型肝炎病毒相关肝硬化的患者预后。
局限与证据强度
该研究为回顾性研究,依赖于倾向评分匹配,这可能无法完全消除混杂因素。由于研究的观察性质和潜在偏倚,证据等级为中等。

Interferon-based HBV function cure therapy reduces hepatocellular carcinoma risk in low-HBsAg chronic hepatitis B patients: a propensity-matched cohort study and dynamic prediction model

Authors: Yu-Rong Gu, Pei-Pei Wang, Qin-Yao Xu, Ting Liu, Hua-Ping Xie, Bao-Er Wu, Yusheng Jie, Xiang-Yong Li, 谢华萍
Original abstract (English)
Background Functional cure through HBsAg loss is a key treatment goal in chronic hepatitis B (CHB), yet its impact on hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) risk remains incompletely characterized, especially when achieved via interferon (IFN)-based therapy. We aimed to evaluate the HCC risk reduction associated with IFN-based therapy in low-HBsAg CHB patients and develop a validated prediction model for this population. Methods We conducted a retrospective cohort study of 3,367 CHB patients with HBsAg <1,500 IU/mL, including 1,780 receiving IFN-based therapy and 1,587 on nucleos(t)ide analogue (NAs) monotherapy. Propensity score matching (PSM) was used to balance baseline characteristics. Cox regression and time-dependent ROC analyses were employed to develop a dynamic nomogram for HCC prediction. Results IFN-based therapy was associated with a significantly lower HCC incidence compared to NAs monotherapy (1.2% vs. 4.9%, p<0.001), with a hazard ratio of 3.03 (95% CI: 1.82–5.06) after PSM. The protective effect was most pronounced in cirrhotic patients (absolute risk reduction 8.8%, NNT = 11). A novel nomogram incorporating treatment modality, AFP, cirrhosis, age, and other variables demonstrated excellent predictive accuracy (5-year AUC: 0.888 in derivation, 0.956 in validation cohorts), outperforming existing HCC prediction models. Conclusions IFN-based therapy significantly reduces HCC risk in low-HBsAg CHB patients, particularly those with cirrhosis. We developed and validated a dynamic prediction model that may facilitate personalized surveillance and treatment decisions in functionally cured patients.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Functional cure through HBsAg loss is a key treatment goal in chronic hepatitis B (CHB), yet its impact on hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) risk remains incompletely characterized, especially when achieved via interferon (IFN)-based therapy.
Design and population
We conducted a retrospective cohort study of 3,367 CHB patients with HBsAg <1,500 IU/mL, including 1,780 receiving IFN-based therapy and 1,587 on nucleos(t)ide analogue (NAs) monotherapy.
Core methods
Propensity score matching (PSM) was used to balance baseline characteristics. Cox regression and time-dependent ROC analyses were employed to develop a dynamic nomogram for HCC prediction.
Main results
IFN-based therapy was associated with a significantly lower HCC incidence compared to NAs monotherapy (1.2% vs. 4.9%, p<0.001), with a hazard ratio of 3.03 (95% CI: 1.82–5.06) after PSM. A novel nomogram incorporating treatment modality, AFP, cirrhosis, age, and other variables demonstrated excellent predictive accuracy (5-year AUC: 0.888 in derivation, 0.956 in validation cohorts), outperforming existing HCC prediction models.
Interpretation and novelty
IFN-based therapy significantly reduces HCC risk in low-HBsAg CHB patients, particularly those with cirrhosis. We developed and validated a dynamic prediction model that may facilitate personalized surveillance and treatment decisions in functionally cured patients.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform personalized surveillance and treatment decisions in functionally cured patients, potentially improving outcomes for those with HBV-related cirrhosis.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is retrospective and relies on propensity score matching, which may not fully eliminate confounding. The evidence is moderate due to the observational nature and potential biases.

Fabry病与乙型肝炎病毒相关IgA肾病并发:以疼痛为前驱的病例报告

Concurrent Fabry disease and Hepatitis-B virus-associated IgA nephropathy: A case report with pain as a precursor
作者： Hui-Wang Zhao, Sen-Yao Xue, Wei Li
摘要中文翻译我们报告了一例35岁男性患者,其同时患有Fabry病和乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)相关的IgA肾病,且多年来被误诊为“风湿病”进行治疗。该患者出现四肢严重灼痛、显微镜下血尿和肾病范围蛋白尿,但肾功能保持正常。自身免疫检测为阴性,但HBV标志物为阳性。肾活检显示以IgA为主的系膜增生性肾小球肾炎,伴有系膜区HBsAg沉积,电镜检查发现足细胞和管型中存在类似髓鞘的包涵体。显著降低的α-半乳糖苷酶A活性、升高的溶酶体GL-3水平以及剪接位点GLA突变确认了Fabry病的诊断。使用肾素-血管紧张素阻断剂、恩替卡韦和酶替代治疗改善了疼痛和蛋白尿,并稳定了肾功能。该病例表明,Fabry病与HBV相关IgA肾病并发的情况较为罕见且容易被误诊,持续性神经病理性疼痛伴蛋白尿应提示考虑代谢性和遗传性肾脏疾病。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在报告一例罕见的Fabry病与HBV相关IgA肾病并发病例,强调在出现持续性神经病理性疼痛和蛋白尿的患者中考虑代谢性和遗传性疾病的必要性。
设计与对象
对一例35岁同时患有Fabry病和HBV相关IgA肾病的男性患者进行了病例报告,该患者多年来被误诊为“风湿病”并接受治疗,且出现严重灼痛、显微镜下血尿和肾病范围蛋白尿,同时保持肾功能正常。
核心方法
进行了自身免疫检测,并评估了HBV标志物。进行了肾活检和电镜检查以评估组织病理学特征,实验室检测通过α-半乳糖苷酶A活性降低、溶酶体GL-3水平升高以及剪接位点GLA突变确认了Fabry病的诊断。
主要结果
肾活检显示以IgA为主的系膜增生性肾小球肾炎,伴有系膜区HBsAg沉积,电镜检查发现足细胞和管型中存在类似髓鞘的包涵体。通过显著降低的α-半乳糖苷酶A活性、升高的溶酶体GL-3水平以及剪接位点GLA突变确认了Fabry病。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为Fabry病与HBV相关IgA肾病并发的罕见病例,强调在持续性神经病理性疼痛和蛋白尿患者中进行细致鉴别诊断的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,持续性神经病理性疼痛伴蛋白尿应提示考虑代谢性和遗传性肾脏疾病,这可能有助于在临床实践中采取更具针对性的诊断方法。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于一例病例报告,可能不具备普遍推广性。证据基于临床观察和实验室检测结果,对所报告的关联提供了中等程度的置信度。

Concurrent Fabry disease and Hepatitis-B virus-associated IgA nephropathy: A case report with pain as a precursor

Authors: Hui-Wang Zhao, Sen-Yao Xue, Wei Li
Original abstract (English)
We report a 35-year-old man with Fabry disease coexisting with Hepatitis-B virus (HBV)-associated IgA nephropathy who had been treated for “rheumatism” for many years. He presented with severe burning pain in his extremities, microscopic hematuria and nephrotic-range proteinuria, while kidney function was preserved. Autoimmune tests were negative, but HBV markers were positive. Renal biopsy showed IgA-dominant mesangial proliferative glomerulonephritis with mesangial HBsAg deposits, and electron microscopy revealed myelin-like inclusions in podocytes and tubules. Markedly reduced α-galactosidase A activity, elevated lyso-GL-3 and a splice-site GLA mutation confirmed Fabry disease. Treatment with renin-angiotensin blockade, entecavir and enzyme replacement improved pain and proteinuria and stabilized renal function. This case illustrates that Fabry disease coexisting with HBV-associated IgA nephropathy is uncommon and easily underdiagnosed, and that persistent neuropathic pain with proteinuria should prompt consideration of metabolic and genetic renal disorders.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to report a rare case of concurrent Fabry disease and HBV-associated IgA nephropathy, highlighting the importance of considering metabolic and genetic disorders in patients presenting with persistent neuropathic pain and proteinuria.
Design and population
A case report was conducted on a 35-year-old man with Fabry disease coexisting with HBV-associated IgA nephropathy, who had been treated for "rheumatism" for many years and presented with severe burning pain, microscopic hematuria, and nephrotic-range proteinuria while maintaining preserved kidney function.
Core methods
Autoimmune tests were performed, and HBV markers were assessed. Renal biopsy and electron microscopy were conducted to evaluate histopathological features, and laboratory tests confirmed Fabry disease through reduced α-galactosidase A activity, elevated lyso-GL-3 levels, and a splice-site GLA mutation.
Main results
Renal biopsy revealed IgA-dominant mesangial proliferative glomerulonephritis with mesangial HBsAg deposits, and electron microscopy showed myelin-like inclusions in podocytes and tubules. Fabry disease was confirmed by markedly reduced α-galactosidase A activity, elevated lyso-GL-3 and a splice-site GLA mutation.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as an illustration of the uncommon coexistence of Fabry disease and HBV-associated IgA nephropathy, emphasizing the need for careful differential diagnosis in patients with persistent neuropathic pain and proteinuria.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that persistent neuropathic pain with proteinuria should prompt consideration of metabolic and genetic renal disorders, potentially informing more targeted diagnostic approaches in clinical practice.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single case report, which may not be generalizable. The evidence is based on clinical observations and laboratory findings, providing a moderate level of confidence in the reported association.

全球抗病毒药物疗法在乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)相关肝细胞癌中的应用不足及其生存结局:一项纳入78项研究和68,243例患者的系统综述和荟萃分析。

Global Underutilization of Antiviral Therapy and Survival Outcomes in Hepatitis B Virus-Related Hepatocellular Carcinoma: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis of 78 Studies and 68,243 Patients.
作者： Sahith Kudaravalli, Taotao Yan, Cheng-Hao Tseng, Yao-Chun Hsu, Xiaohe Li, Yuankai Wu, Bowu Chen, Xiangrong Gao, Sicheng Gao, Danfeng Ren
摘要中文翻译使用核苷(酸)类似物进行抗病毒药物治疗被推荐用于所有具有病毒血症的乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)相关肝硬化的患者,无论其病毒载量或肝酶水平如何。然而,肝细胞癌(HCC)并不是美国HBV 治疗指南中的治疗标准。尽管之前的研究报告指出,在HBV相关HCC患者中,抗病毒药物治疗与改善生存率相关,但目前仍缺乏明确的指南。为了指导指南制定,我们估计了抗病毒药物治疗率及其与生存率和HCC复发率之间的关联。我们按照PRISMA指南,对2006年至2024年间发表的、报告了HBV相关HCC成人患者抗病毒药物治疗率、生存率和/或HCC复发率的研究进行了系统综述和荟萃分析。使用随机效应模型汇总研究估计值。初始共有4556项研究,其中178项进行了全文审查,最终分析了78项(共68,243名患者)。汇总的抗病毒药物治疗率(52项研究,41,345名患者)为53.1%。在报告了HCC前抗病毒药物治疗启动的研究中,17.2%的HBV相关HCC患者在HCC发生前接受了治疗。接受治疗(与未接受治疗相比)的患者3年和5年总生存率(OS)更高(80.4% vs. 46.8%,两者p < 0.01)。在非活动性HCC研究中,5年OS为78.0% vs. 56.4%(p < 0.01),而在活动性HCC研究中,5年OS为48.2% vs. 22.6%(p = 0.14)。在多变量荟萃回归分析中,抗病毒药物治疗仍与改善5年OS独立相关。HCC复发率没有显著差异。抗病毒药物治疗与改善长期生存率相关,但在HBV相关HCC中仍被低估使用。这些发现突显了护理中的关键差距,并强调了在HBV相关HCC患者中更早和更一致地启动抗病毒药物治疗的必要性。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述关注全球在乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)相关肝细胞癌(HCC)患者中对抗病毒药物疗法的应用不足及其对生存结局的影响。核心问题是抗病毒药物疗法是否能改善HBV相关HCC患者的生存率,以及其应用在不同人群中的差异。
证据基础与方法
本综述是一项系统分析和荟萃分析,纳入了2006年至2024年间发表的78项研究,共涉及68,243例患者。研究遵循PRISMA指南进行筛选,采用随机效应模型合并各研究的估计值。初始检索共获得4556项研究,其中178项进行了全文审阅。
共识与结论
使用核苷(酸)类似物的抗病毒药物疗法与HBV相关HCC患者的长期生存率改善相关。接受治疗的患者3年和5年总体生存率(OS)均高于未接受治疗的患者。然而,抗病毒药物疗法在临床实践中仍应用不足,仅有17.2%的患者在HCC发生前接受该疗法。
争议与缺口
治疗组与未治疗组在HCC复发率方面无显著差异。此外,综述还指出在护理方面存在关键缺口,建议需要更早且更一致地启动抗病毒药物疗法。证据还表明,抗病毒药物疗法与生存率之间的关联可能因HCC的活动性而有所不同。
科研与实践启示
研究结果强调需要更新临床指南,将HCC纳入抗病毒药物治疗的考虑因素。还需要进一步研究以确定HBV相关HCC中抗病毒药物疗法的最佳时机和持续时间,并解决不同HCC亚型中治疗结局的差异性。}}.{

Global Underutilization of Antiviral Therapy and Survival Outcomes in Hepatitis B Virus-Related Hepatocellular Carcinoma: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis of 78 Studies and 68,243 Patients.

Authors: Sahith Kudaravalli, Taotao Yan, Cheng-Hao Tseng, Yao-Chun Hsu, Xiaohe Li, Yuankai Wu, Bowu Chen, Xiangrong Gao, Sicheng Gao, Danfeng Ren
Original abstract (English)
Antiviral therapy with nucleos(t)ide analogues is recommended for patients with viremic hepatitis B virus (HBV)-related cirrhosis regardless of levels of viremia or liver enzymes. However, hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) is not an antiviral treatment criterion in the U.S. HBV treatment guidelines, though prior studies have reported improved survival with antiviral treatment in HBV-related HCC. To inform guidelines, we estimated antiviral treatment rates and their association with survival and HCC recurrence rates in HBV-related HCC. We conducted a systematic review and meta-analysis of studies published between 2006 and 2024 that reported antiviral treatment, survival, and/or HCC recurrence rates in adults with HBV-related HCC, following PRISMA guidelines. Random-effects models pooled study estimates. Of the initial 4556 studies, 178 underwent full-text review, and 78 (68,243 patients) were analysed. The pooled antiviral treatment rate (52 studies, 41,345 patients) was 53.1%. In studies reporting pre-HCC antiviral initiation, 17.2% of HBV-related HCC patients received therapy before diagnosis. Treated (vs untreated) patients had higher 3- and 5-year OS (80.4% vs. 46.8%, both p < 0.01). Five-year OS was 78.0% vs. 56.4% in inactive HCC (p < 0.01) and 48.2% vs. 22.6% in active HCC (p = 0.14). Antiviral therapy remained independently associated with improved 5-year OS in multivariable meta-regression. There was no significant difference in HCC recurrence rates. Antiviral therapy is associated with improved long-term survival but remains underutilized in HBV-related HCC. These findings highlight a critical gap in care and underscore the need for earlier and more consistent antiviral initiation in patients with HBV-related HCC.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the global underutilization of antiviral therapy in patients with hepatitis B virus (HBV)-related hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) and its impact on survival outcomes. The central question is whether antiviral therapy improves survival in HBV-related HCC and how its utilization varies across populations.
Evidence base and method
The review is a systematic analysis and meta-analysis of 78 studies involving 68,243 patients, published between 2006 and 2024. The studies were selected following PRISMA guidelines, and random-effects models were used to pool study estimates. The initial search identified 4556 studies, of which 178 underwent full-text review.
Consensus and conclusions
Antiviral therapy with nucleos(t)ide analogues is associated with improved long-term survival in HBV-related HCC. Treated patients had higher 3- and 5-year overall survival (OS) rates compared to untreated patients. However, the use of antiviral therapy remains underutilized in clinical practice, with only 17.2% of patients receiving it before HCC diagnosis.
Controversies and gaps
There is no significant difference in HCC recurrence rates between treated and untreated patients. Additionally, the review highlights a critical gap in care, suggesting that earlier and more consistent antiviral initiation is needed. The evidence also indicates that the association between antiviral therapy and survival may vary depending on the activity of HCC.
Research and practice implications
The findings emphasize the need for updating clinical guidelines to include HCC as an antiviral treatment criterion. Further research is required to determine the optimal timing and duration of antiviral therapy in HBV-related HCC and to address the variability in treatment outcomes across different HCC subtypes.

HBeAg阴性慢性乙型肝炎中有限与无限核苷(酸)类似物治疗的对比:随机试验的荟萃分析

Finite Versus Indefinite Nucleos(t)ide Analogue Therapy in HBeAg‐Negative Chronic Hepatitis B: Meta‐Analysis of Randomized Trials.
作者： Demver P Gomez, Wilmyr F Hababag
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本研究探讨有限疗程与连续疗程核苷(酸)类似物(NA)治疗在HBeAg阴性慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)成人中的疗效和安全性。核心问题是有限疗程是否能增加HBsAg丢失的可能性,同时维持病毒抑制。
证据基础与方法
本综述纳入了四项平行组随机对照试验(RCTs),比较非肝硬化、病毒抑制的HBeAg阴性成人中NA停药与继续治疗的效果。两名评审员独立筛选研究并使用Cochrane RoB 2评估偏倚风险。采用随机效应Mantel-Haenszel荟萃分析,结合Hartung-Knapp调整和Paule-Mandel估计方法,合并风险比(RRs)及其95%置信区间(CIs)。证据的确定性通过GRADE方法进行评估。
共识与结论
与连续治疗相比,有限疗程的NA治疗可能增加HBsAg丢失的概率,尽管证据确定性较低。研究发现,停药与HBsAg丢失的风险比为5.02(95% CI:0.47-53.47),相当于每1000名患者约有53例额外的HBsAg丢失。然而,有限疗程显著增加了病毒学复发(RR 23.09)和再次治疗(RR 21.94)的风险,同时降低了持续停药后的病毒学应答率(RR 0.46)。未报告肝功能失代偿或严重不良事件。
争议与缺口
国际指南对有限疗程与连续疗程NA治疗的推荐存在分歧。关于HBsAg丢失的证据确定性较低,且置信区间较宽,表明效果估计存在不确定性。此外,本研究未涉及长期结局或有限疗程对乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)相关肝细胞癌或肝硬化的潜在影响。
科研与实践启示
需要进一步研究以明确有限疗程NA治疗的长期安全性和有效性,特别是其对HBV相关肝细胞癌和肝硬化的潜在影响。研究结果表明,有限疗程可能在专家中心作为实现功能性治愈的一种选择性策略,但需要更多数据支持其更广泛的应用。

Finite Versus Indefinite Nucleos(t)ide Analogue Therapy in HBeAg‐Negative Chronic Hepatitis B: Meta‐Analysis of Randomized Trials.

Authors: Demver P Gomez, Wilmyr F Hababag
Original abstract (English)
Continuous nucleos(t)ide analogue (NA) therapy for chronic hepatitis B (CHB) effectively maintains viral suppression but rarely results in functional cure. Finite NA therapy has been proposed to increase rates of HBsAg loss; however, international guidelines provide conflicting recommendations. This study compares the efficacy and safety of finite versus continuous NA therapy in HBeAg-negative adults using randomized controlled trials. We included parallel-group randomized controlled trials (RCTs) comparing nucleos(t)ide analogue (NA) cessation versus continuation in non-cirrhotic, virally suppressed HBeAg-negative adults. Two reviewers independently screened studies and assessed risk of bias using Cochrane RoB 2. Random-effects Mantel-Haenszel meta-analyses with Hartung-Knapp adjustment and Paule-Mandel estimation were used to pool risk ratios (RRs) with 95% confidence intervals (CIs). Certainty of evidence was assessed using the GRADE approach. Four RCTs (n = 357) met inclusion criteria. Discontinuation increased HBsAg loss vs. continuation (RR 5.02, 95% CI: 0.47-53.47; I 2 = 21.8%; low certainty), corresponding to ~53 additional losses per 1000 patients. However, finite therapy markedly increased virologic relapse (RR 23.09) and retreatment (RR 21.94) while reducing sustained off-therapy virologic response (RR 0.46). Post-cessation ALT flares were frequent but self-limited or resolved with retreatment. No hepatic decompensation or serious adverse events occurred. In monitored, non-cirrhotic adults, finite NA therapy may increase the probability of HBsAg loss despite predictable relapse and retreatment. With no evidence of severe harm, these findings support finite therapy as a selective, patient-centered strategy toward functional cure in expert centers.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This study investigates the efficacy and safety of finite versus continuous nucleos(t)ide analogue (NA) therapy in HBeAg-negative adults with chronic hepatitis B (CHB). The central question is whether finite therapy increases the likelihood of HBsAg loss while maintaining viral suppression.
Evidence base and method
The review includes four parallel-group randomized controlled trials (RCTs) comparing NA cessation versus continuation in non-cirrhotic, virally suppressed HBeAg-negative adults. Two reviewers independently screened studies and assessed risk of bias using Cochrane RoB 2. Random-effects Mantel-Haenszel meta-analyses with Hartung-Knapp adjustment and Paule-Mandel estimation were used to pool risk ratios (RRs) with 95% confidence intervals (CIs). Certainty of evidence was evaluated using the GRADE approach.
Consensus and conclusions
Finite NA therapy may increase the probability of HBsAg loss compared to continuous therapy, although the evidence is of low certainty. The study found that discontinuation of therapy was associated with a risk ratio of 5.02 (95% CI: 0.47-53.47) for HBsAg loss, corresponding to approximately 53 additional losses per 1000 patients. However, finite therapy significantly increased virologic relapse (RR 23.09) and retreatment (RR 21.94) while reducing sustained off-therapy virologic response (RR 0.46). No hepatic decompensation or serious adverse events were reported.
Controversies and gaps
International guidelines provide conflicting recommendations on finite versus continuous NA therapy. The evidence for HBsAg loss is of low certainty, and the wide confidence interval suggests uncertainty in the effect estimate. Additionally, the study does not address long-term outcomes or the impact of finite therapy on HBV-associated hepatocellular carcinoma or cirrhosis.
Research and practice implications
Further research is needed to clarify the long-term safety and efficacy of finite NA therapy, particularly in relation to HBV-associated hepatocellular carcinoma and cirrhosis. The findings suggest that finite therapy could be considered as a selective strategy in expert centers for achieving functional cure, but more data are required to support its broader implementation.

针对乙型肝炎病毒表面抗原清除的靶向策略:从机制探索到临床转化的进展综述

Hepatitis B surface antigen-targeting strategies for clearance: A review of progress from mechanistic exploration to clinical translation
作者： Li Wang
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎病毒表面抗原(HBsAg)的持续存在阻碍了慢性乙型肝炎患者的功能性治愈。抑制或清除HBsAg并打破免疫耐受、恢复免疫应答的策略成为免疫治疗导向临床治愈的关键组成部分。针对HBsAg的药物包括单克隆抗体、小干扰RNA、反义寡核苷酸、基因编辑技术、衣壳组装调节剂以及靶向分泌途径的治疗药物。免疫检查点抑制剂和其他基于免疫的清除方法也包含在这一战略框架中。这些药物靶向HBsAg生命周期及其来源模板,包括共价闭合环状DNA(共价闭合环状DNA)和整合在宿主基因组中的乙型肝炎病毒DNA。本文通过综合预临床研究和临床试验的最新信息,回顾了这些策略,总结了其机制和疗效,讨论了组合策略的挑战与前景,并旨在为功能性治愈研究提供科学依据。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
综述聚焦于清除慢性乙型肝炎患者中的乙型肝炎病毒表面抗原(HBsAg)的策略,旨在通过抑制或清除HBsAg并打破免疫耐受、恢复免疫应答来实现功能性治愈。
证据基础与方法
综述综合了预临床研究和临床试验的最新信息,总结了针对HBsAg的各种药物的机制和疗效,包括单克隆抗体、小干扰RNA、反义寡核苷酸、基因编辑技术、衣壳组装调节剂以及靶向分泌途径的治疗药物。它还涵盖了免疫检查点抑制剂和其他基于免疫的清除方法。
共识与结论
综述强调,靶向HBsAg生命周期及其来源模板,如共价闭合环状DNA(共价闭合环状DNA)和整合在宿主基因组中的乙型肝炎病毒DNA,是免疫治疗导向临床治愈的关键组成部分。它讨论了这些策略组合的挑战与前景。
争议与缺口
综述指出不同药物在疗效上的异质性以及策略组合的挑战。它还提到需要进一步研究以解决未明确的机制并改善基于免疫的清除方法的疗效。
科研与实践启示
综述建议了具体的研究优先事项,包括开发更有效的针对HBsAg及其来源模板的药物,以及探索组合疗法以增强免疫应答并实现功能性治愈。

Hepatitis B surface antigen-targeting strategies for clearance: A review of progress from mechanistic exploration to clinical translation

Authors: Li Wang
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) persistence hinders the achievement of a functional cure in patients with chronic hepatitis B. Strategies to suppress or eliminate HBsAg and break the host immune tolerance while restoring immune response become key components of immune therapy-directed clinical cure.Agents that target HBsAg include monoclonal antibodies, small interfering RNAs, antisense oligonucleotides, gene editing technologies, capsid assembly modulators, and therapeutic agents targeting secretion pathways.Immune checkpoint inhibitors and other immune-based clearance approaches are also included within this strategic framework.These agents target the HBsAg lifecycle and its source templates, including covalently closed circular DNA and integrated hepatitis B virus DNA in the host genome.This article reviews these strategies by synthesizing the latest information from preclinical studies and clinical trials, summarises their mechanism and efficacy, discusses challenges and prospects for combinations, and aims to provide a scientific basis for chronic hepatitis B curative research.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on strategies to clear Hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) in patients with chronic hepatitis B, aiming to achieve a functional cure by suppressing or eliminating HBsAg and breaking host immune tolerance while restoring immune response.
Evidence base and method
The review synthesizes the latest information from preclinical studies and clinical trials, summarizing the mechanisms and efficacy of various agents targeting HBsAg, including monoclonal antibodies, small interfering RNAs, antisense oligonucleotides, gene editing technologies, capsid assembly modulators, and therapeutic agents targeting secretion pathways. It also includes immune checkpoint inhibitors and other immune-based clearance approaches.
Consensus and conclusions
The review highlights that targeting HBsAg lifecycle and its source templates, such as covalently closed circular DNA and integrated hepatitis B virus DNA in the host genome, is a key component of immune therapy-directed clinical cure. It discusses the challenges and prospects for combinations of these strategies.
Controversies and gaps
The review identifies heterogeneity in the efficacy of different agents and challenges in combining strategies. It also notes the need for further research to address unresolved mechanisms and improve the outcomes of immune-based clearance approaches.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests specific priorities for research, including the development of more effective agents targeting HBsAg and its source templates, as well as the exploration of combination therapies to enhance immune response and achieve a functional cure.

患者报告的治疗负担、沟通和持续治疗意愿在接受附加peginterferon alfa-2b治疗的慢性乙型肝炎患者中的研究:一项横断面研究

Patient-reported treatment burden, communication, and persistence intention during add-on peginterferon alfa-2b therapy for chronic hepatitis B: a cross-sectional study
作者： Ning Han, Huan Liu, Li-Bo Yan, Hong Tang
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本研究调查了在接受附加peginterferon alfa-2b(Peg-IFN)治疗的慢性乙型肝炎(慢性乙型肝炎)患者中,患者报告的与治疗负担、沟通和持续治疗意愿相关的因素。核心问题是不良反应负担和心理压力是否与中断治疗的想法相关,以及治疗信念和医患沟通是否与持续治疗意愿相关。
证据基础与方法
本横断面研究使用了匿名在线调查,并基于结果分析了特定人群。主要的持续分析包括289名仍在接受Peg-IFN治疗的受访者,而中断相关分析包括所有412名符合条件的受访者。主要结果包括受访者是否因不良反应想要停止Peg-IFN治疗,以及一个包含四项的持续/价值意愿评分。心理压力通过患者健康问卷-4(PHQ-4)进行评估。
共识与结论
不良反应负担和当前心理压力与不良反应相关中断治疗的想法有关。相比之下,治疗信念和医患沟通与仍在接受治疗的患者更高的持续/价值意愿相关。
争议与缺口
本研究未探讨HBV基因型对治疗负担或持续治疗意愿的潜在影响。此外,它也未研究其他因素如社会经济状况、文化影响或治疗依从策略在塑造患者行为中的作用。
科研与实践启示
需要前瞻性研究来评估结构化教育、症状和压力筛查以及分级随访是否能提高治疗治疗的完成率。这些干预措施对于支持患者应对Peg-IFN治疗的挑战可能是关键的。

Patient-reported treatment burden, communication, and persistence intention during add-on peginterferon alfa-2b therapy for chronic hepatitis B: a cross-sectional study

Authors: Ning Han, Huan Liu, Li-Bo Yan, Hong Tang
Original abstract (English)
Background Add-on peginterferon alfa-2b (Peg-IFN) may help reduce hepatitis B surface antigen levels and achieve a functional cure in selected patients with chronic hepatitis B (CHB), but the treatment burden can make a finite course difficult to complete. We examined the patient-reported factors associated with thoughts of discontinuation and intentions to persist with treatment during add-on Peg-IFN therapy. Methods This anonymous cross-sectional online survey used outcome-specific analytic populations. All 412 eligible respondents were included in discontinuation-related analyses, and the primary persistence analysis included 289 respondents who were still receiving Peg-IFN. The main outcomes included whether respondents had ever wanted to stop Peg-IFN due to adverse effects and a four-item persistence/value intention score. Psychological distress was assessed using the Patient Health Questionnaire-4 (PHQ-4). Results Among 412 eligible respondents, 175 (42.5%) had wanted to stop Peg-IFN because of adverse effects, 136 (33.0%) had asked a physician about dose reduction or discontinuation, and 39 (9.5%) reported actual premature discontinuation. In the adjusted analysis, adverse-effect burden (odds ratio 1.86 per standard deviation (SD), 95% confidence interval 1.37–2.51) and the PHQ-4 score (odds ratio 1.76, 95% confidence interval 1.28–2.41) were associated with a history of adverse-effect-related discontinuation thoughts. Among 289 currently treated respondents, treatment belief ( β = 0.280, 95% confidence interval 0.198–0.362) and physician–patient communication ( β = 0.098, 95% confidence interval 0.017–0.179) were associated with higher persistence/value intention, while the PHQ-4 score was inversely associated with this outcome ( β = −0.081, 95% confidence interval −0.148 to −0.013). Conclusion Adverse-effect burden and current psychological distress were associated with a history of adverse-effect-related discontinuation thoughts, whereas treatment belief and physician–patient communication were associated with higher persistence/value intention among patients still receiving therapy. Prospective studies are needed to evaluate whether structured education, symptom and distress screening, and tiered follow-up improve the rates of treatment completion.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The study investigates patient-reported factors influencing treatment burden, communication, and persistence intention during add-on peginterferon alfa-2b (Peg-IFN) therapy for chronic hepatitis B (CHB). The central question is whether adverse-effect burden and psychological distress are associated with discontinuation thoughts, and whether treatment belief and physician-patient communication are linked to persistence intention.
Evidence base and method
This cross-sectional study used an anonymous online survey with outcome-specific analytic populations. The primary persistence analysis included 289 respondents still receiving Peg-IFN, while discontinuation-related analyses included all 412 eligible respondents. The main outcomes were whether respondents wanted to stop Peg-IFN due to adverse effects and a four-item persistence/value intention score. Psychological distress was assessed using the Patient Health Questionnaire-4 (PHQ-4).
Consensus and conclusions
Adverse-effect burden and current psychological distress were associated with a history of adverse-effect-related discontinuation thoughts. In contrast, treatment belief and physician-patient communication were linked to higher persistence/value intention among patients still receiving therapy.
Controversies and gaps
The study does not address the potential impact of HBV genotypes on treatment burden or persistence intention. It also does not explore the role of other factors such as socioeconomic status, cultural influences, or treatment adherence strategies in shaping patient behavior.
Research and practice implications
Prospective studies are needed to evaluate whether structured education, symptom and distress screening, and tiered follow-up improve the rates of treatment completion. These interventions could be critical for supporting patients through the challenges of Peg-IFN therapy.

乙型肝炎病毒和乙型肝炎病毒/丁型肝炎病毒检测:意大利当前状况与展望

Testing for hepatitis C virus and hepatitis B virus/hepatitis D virus: Current scenarios and perspectives in Italy
作者： Francesca Colapietro, Maurizia Rossana Brunetto, Nicola Coppola, Stefano Fagiuoli, Pietro Lampertico
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述聚焦于乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染、基因型及其相关疾病。它考察了意大利当前HBV的检测状况和管理策略,特别强调了如何通过协调努力和针对性干预来提高HBV的检测和治疗连接性。核心问题是:如何通过协调努力和针对性干预提高意大利病毒性肝炎的管理效果,特别是针对HBV。
证据基础与方法
本综述是一篇专家意见文章,总结了意大利三级医疗机构在医院内进行HBV、HCV和HDV检测的经验。它并未报告特定数据库、搜索日期、纳入标准或元分析方法。综述基于作者的专业知识和在意大利医疗环境中的观察实践。
共识与结论
综述强调了改善医院单位与全科医生之间协调的必要性,实施集中报告系统,并扩大高风险人群的检测。它还强调了为公共成瘾治疗服务和监狱提供资源以增强HBV检测和管理的重要性。这些措施被提议以帮助意大利在未来十年内实现消除HCV感染和控制HBV/HDV感染的目标。
争议与缺口
综述未报告与HBV相关的具体争议或证据缺口。它专注于识别改进检测和管理的可行途径,而未涉及与HBV相关的矛盾发现或未解决的机制。
科研与实践启示
综述提出了针对研究、监测和实践的具体优先事项,包括加强医院单位与全科医生之间的协调,实施集中报告系统,增加公共成瘾治疗服务和监狱中的检测,推广高风险人群的筛查,并启动全国性的病毒性肝炎意识宣传活动。这些建议旨在提高意大利HBV的管理效果。

Testing for hepatitis C virus and hepatitis B virus/hepatitis D virus: Current scenarios and perspectives in Italy

Authors: Francesca Colapietro, Maurizia Rossana Brunetto, Nicola Coppola, Stefano Fagiuoli, Pietro Lampertico
Original abstract (English)
Liver disease accounts for approximately 4% of all deaths worldwide, and viral hepatitis affects more than 300 million people.The World Health Organization aims to reduce new hepatitis infections by 90% and deaths by 65% by 2030 through vaccination and screening programs.In Italy, screening for hepatitis C virus (HCV) benefits from dedicated government funding, whereas testing for hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis D virus (HDV) is supported by recommendations from scientific societies but does not benefit from dedicated national public health programmes.This expert-opinion review summarises the experience of tertiary-level Italian centres actively engaged in hospitalbased testing for HCV, HBV, and HDV and identifies practical pathways to improve testing and linkage to care.This article presents proposals to improve the effectiveness of viral hepatitis management in Italy by: (1) Strengthening coordination between hospital units and integrating information systems between general practitioners and hospitals;(2) Implementing a centralised reporting system and a structured referral network to ensure that patients with positive test results receive appropriate therapies in a timely manner; (3) Providing the resources needed to increase testing in public addiction treatment services (Servizi per le Dipendenze) and prisons; (4) Promoting screening in high-risk populations, including vulnerable and older populations; and (5) Implementing a national awareness campaign on viral hepatitis.These measures could help Italy make concrete progress towards eliminating HCV infection and controlling HBV/HDV infection within the next decade.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection, genotypes, and associated diseases. It examines the current testing scenarios and management strategies for HBV in Italy, with an emphasis on improving testing and linkage to care. The central question is how to enhance the effectiveness of viral hepatitis management in Italy, particularly for HBV, through coordinated efforts and targeted interventions.
Evidence base and method
The review is an expert-opinion piece summarizing the experience of tertiary-level Italian centers involved in hospital-based testing for HBV, HCV, and HDV. It does not report on specific databases, search dates, eligibility criteria, or meta-analysis methods. The review is based on the authors' expertise and the practices observed in Italian healthcare settings.
Consensus and conclusions
The review highlights the need for improved coordination between hospital units and general practitioners, the implementation of centralized reporting systems, and the expansion of testing in high-risk populations. It also emphasizes the importance of providing resources to public addiction treatment services and prisons to enhance HBV testing and management. These measures are proposed to help Italy achieve its goals of eliminating HCV infection and controlling HBV/HDV infection within the next decade.
Controversies and gaps
The review does not report on controversies or evidence gaps specific to HBV. It focuses on identifying practical pathways to improve testing and management without addressing conflicting findings or unresolved mechanisms related to HBV.
Research and practice implications
The review proposes specific priorities for research, surveillance, and practice, including strengthening coordination between hospital units and general practitioners, implementing a centralized reporting system, increasing testing in public addiction treatment services and prisons, promoting screening in high-risk populations, and launching a national awareness campaign on viral hepatitis. These recommendations aim to improve the effectiveness of HBV management in Italy.

摘要 C081:移民史及与适应相关因素与韩国裔美国人慢性乙型肝炎患者健康相关生活质量及抑郁症状的关系

Abstract C081: Immigration history and acculturation-related factors in relation to health-related quality of life and depressive symptoms among Korean American patients with Chronic Hepatitis B (CHB)
作者： Eunji Kim, Ann Carroll Klassen, Giyoung Lee, Amy Auchincloss, Sandra L. Bloom, Stephanie Ewing, Mi-Mi Chang, Kyunghee Koh, Hee-Soon Juon, 김은지
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景:慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)主要影响亚洲裔美国人,尤其是韩国裔美国人,并增加长期并发症(包括肝癌)的风险。移民史(如抵达美国时的年龄/人生阶段)以及与适应相关因素(如英语熟练程度和文化认同)可能影响患有CHB的人群的身心健康相关生活质量,但这些关系尚未得到充分研究。我们探讨了这些因素是否与韩国裔美国人慢性乙型肝炎患者的身心健康相关生活质量及抑郁症状相关。方法:我们使用了2022-2023年从费城和洛杉矶两家临床机构收集的365名韩国裔美国人慢性乙型肝炎患者的基线调查数据。结果:参与者平均年龄为60.1岁,其中55.6%为男性,且大多数为出生在国外(82.9%),大部分在抵达美国时处于不同年龄段。总体平均的SF-12v2身体成分总结(PCS)得分、心理成分总结(MCS)得分和PHQ-9抑郁症状得分分别为48.56±7.25、47.65±7.00和3.66±4.16。抵达美国时的年龄/人生阶段与PCS显著相关(p≤0.001),但与MCS或PHQ-9无显著相关性。PCS得分最高的是美国出生的参与者(53.81±4.72)以及在童年或青少年时期抵达的参与者(51.99±5.28),而18-34岁成人抵达的参与者(48.07±7.29)和35岁及以上成人抵达的参与者(47.16±7.56)得分较低。英语熟练程度与PCS(p≤0.001)、MCS(p≤0.05)和PHQ-9(p≤0.05)均相关;英语熟练程度差或没有的参与者PCS得分最低(45.68±8.07),PHQ-9得分最高(4.48±4.89)。文化认同与PCS相关(p<0.001),其中主要或非常西方化的参与者PCS得分最高(53.35±3.91),而非常或主要韩国化的参与者PCS得分最低(47.66±7.48)。多变量分析将探讨这些关系在调整社会人口学因素后是否仍然存在。结论:移民史和与适应相关因素最一致地与身体相关的健康生活质量相关。英语熟练程度也与心理健康相关生活质量及抑郁症状相关。这些初步发现强调了为亚洲裔美国人社区提供文化及语言适应性支持在慢性乙型肝炎治疗和肝癌预防中的重要性。引用格式:Eunji Kim,Ann Klassen,Giyoung Lee,Amy Auchincloss,Sandra Bloom,Stephanie Ewing,Mimi Chang,Kyunghee Koh,Hee-Soon Juon. 韩国裔美国人慢性乙型肝炎患者移民史及与适应相关因素与健康相关生活质量及抑郁症状的关系 [摘要]. 出处:第19届AACR癌症健康差异科学会议论文集;2026年10月18-21日;佐治亚州亚特兰大市。费城(PA):AACR;Cancer Epidemiol Biomarkers Prev 2026;35(10 Suppl):摘要编号C081。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本研究探讨移民史及与适应相关因素如何影响韩国裔美国人慢性乙型肝炎患者的身心健康相关生活质量及抑郁症状。
证据基础与方法
一项2022–2023年在费城和洛杉矶两家临床机构进行的横断面调查,收集了365名韩国裔美国人慢性乙型肝炎患者的资料,测量了SF-12v2 PCS/MCS和PHQ-9得分。
共识与结论
抵达美国时的年龄较大和较高的英语熟练程度与更好的身体相关健康生活质量一致相关;英语熟练程度也与更好的心理健康相关生活质量及较低的抑郁症状相关。文化认同在身体相关健康方面也表现出类似模式。
争议与缺口
在未调整分析中发现了相关性;多变量分析尚未完成,横断面设计限制了因果推论。
科研与实践启示
这些发现强调了为亚洲裔美国人社区提供文化及语言适应性支持在慢性乙型肝炎治疗和肝癌预防中的必要性。

Abstract C081: Immigration history and acculturation-related factors in relation to health-related quality of life and depressive symptoms among Korean American patients with Chronic Hepatitis B (CHB)

Authors: Eunji Kim, Ann Carroll Klassen, Giyoung Lee, Amy Auchincloss, Sandra L. Bloom, Stephanie Ewing, Mi-Mi Chang, Kyunghee Koh, Hee-Soon Juon, 김은지
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background: Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) disproportionately affects Asian Americans, especially Korean Americans, and increases risk for long-term complications, including liver cancer. Immigration history, such as age/life stage at U.S. arrival, and acculturation-related factors, such as English proficiency and cultural identity, may shape physical and mental health-related quality of life among individuals living with CHB, but these relationships remain understudied. We examined whether these factors are associated with physical and mental health-related quality of life, and depressive symptoms among Korean American patients with CHB. Methods: We used baseline survey data collected in 2022-2023 from 365 Korean American CHB patients receiving care at two clinical settings in Philadelphia and Los Angeles. Outcomes were the SF-12v2 Physical Component Summary (PCS) and Mental Component Summary (MCS) scores, and the Patient Health Questionnaire-9 (PHQ-9) depressive-symptom score. Results: Participants had a mean age of 60.1 years and were 55.6% male and predominantly foreign-born (82.9%), with most arriving in the U.S. Overall mean PCS, MCS, and PHQ-9 scores were 48.56±7.25, 47.65±7.00, and 3.66±4.16, respectively. Age/life stage at arrival was significantly associated with PCS (p≤0.001), but not MCS or PHQ-9. PCS was highest among U.S.-born participants (53.81±4.72) and those who arrived during childhood or adolescence (51.99±5.28), and lower among adult arrivals ages 18–34 (48.07±7.29) and ages 35 years or older (47.16±7.56). English proficiency was associated with PCS (p≤0.001), MCS (p≤0.05), and PHQ-9 (p≤0.05); participants with poor or no English proficiency had the lowest PCS (45.68±8.07) and highest PHQ-9 scores (4.48±4.89). Cultural identity was associated with PCS (p<0.001), with the highest PCS among mostly or very Westernized participants (53.35±3.91) and the lowest PCS among very/mostly Korean participants (47.66±7.48). Multivariable analyses will examine whether these relationships remain after adjustment for sociodemographic factors. Conclusions: Immigration history and acculturation-related factors were most consistently associated with physical health-related quality of life. English proficiency was also related to mental health-related quality of life and depressive symptoms. These preliminary findings highlight the value of culturally and linguistically responsive support in CHB care and liver cancer prevention for Asian American communities. Citation Format: Eunji Kim, Ann Klassen, Giyoung Lee, Amy Auchincloss, Sandra Bloom, Stephanie Ewing, Mimi Chang, Kyunghee Koh, Hee-Soon Juon. Immigration history and acculturation-related factors in relation to health-related quality of life and depressive symptoms among Korean American patients with Chronic Hepatitis B (CHB) [abstract]. In: Proceedings of the 19th AACR Conference on the Science of Cancer Health Disparities; 2026 Oct 18-21; Atlanta, GA. Philadelphia (PA): AACR; Cancer Epidemiol Biomarkers Prev 2026;35(10 Suppl):Abstract nr C081.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The study investigates how immigration history and acculturation-related factors influence physical and mental health-related quality of life and depressive symptoms among Korean American patients with chronic hepatitis B.
Evidence base and method
A cross-sectional survey of 365 Korean American CHB patients conducted in 2022–2023 at two clinical sites in Philadelphia and Los Angeles, measuring SF‐12v2 PCS/MCS and PHQ‐9 scores.
Consensus and conclusions
Older age at arrival and higher English proficiency were consistently linked to better physical health‐related quality of life; English proficiency also correlated with better mental health‐related quality of life and lower depressive symptoms. Cultural identity showed a similar pattern for physical health.
Controversies and gaps
Associations were identified in unadjusted analyses; multivariable analyses are pending, and the cross‐sectional design limits causal inference.
Research and practice implications
Findings underscore the need for culturally and linguistically tailored support in CHB care and liver cancer prevention for Asian American communities.

一项研究显示,不只是母亲会增加未接种疫苗婴儿患乙型肝炎的风险 - MedPage Today

Not Just Moms Who Put Unvaccinated Infants at Risk of Hepatitis B, Study Shows - MedPage Today
新闻简报一项在2026年IDWeek年会上发表的研究显示,在5岁以下儿童中,1.3%生活在有活跃乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染的户家中,其中58.2%的这些儿童通过非母系接触感染了HBV。由Karen Jacobson,MD,MPH领导的这项研究指出,即使疫苗接种率很高,跳过所有新生儿的HBV疫苗接种剂量仍可能增加可预防感染、肝癌和死亡的风险。研究发现,4.2%通过非母系家庭接触感染HBV的儿童在30天内未接种疫苗。虽然95%的出生接触婴儿接受了HBV检测,但只有3.6%的家庭接触儿童接受了检测。Jacobson强调,不应忽视非母系接触,因为它们可能导致慢性HBV感染,带来终身后果。美国疾病控制与预防中心(CDC)的免疫实践咨询委员会(ACIP)投票决定不再推荐对HBV阴性母亲所生婴儿进行普遍的出生疫苗接种,而是倡导共同决策。批评者警告称,这一变化可能导致HBV感染显著增加,一项建模研究预测慢性感染率将增加83%。该研究还指出了一些局限性,包括地址数据可能不准确,以及未隶属于KPNC的HBV阳性家庭接触者可能被低估。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该研究的发现是在2026年IDWeek年会上报告的,发表日期为2026年10月21日。分析的数据截止到2026年3月。
地点与对象
该研究关注的是居住在 Kaiser Permanente Northern California(KPNC)的5岁以下儿童,特别是那些生活在至少有一名活跃HBV感染者家庭中的儿童。
事件
一项回顾性研究显示,在5岁以下儿童中,1.3%生活在有活跃HBV感染的户家中,其中58.2%的这些儿童通过非母系接触感染了HBV。该研究由Karen Jacobson,MD,MPH在IDWeek年会上发表。
规模、影响与风险
在通过非母系家庭接触感染HBV的儿童中,4.2%在30天内未接种疫苗。研究指出,家庭接触可能导致慢性HBV感染、肝癌和死亡,尤其是在疫苗接种率下降的情况下。它还提到,95%的出生接触婴儿接受了HBV检测,而只有3.6%的家庭接触儿童接受了检测。
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究分析了截至2026年3月的实验室和疫苗接种数据。它承认了一些局限性,例如地址数据可能不准确,以及未隶属于KPNC的HBV阳性家庭接触者可能被低估。美国疾病控制与预防中心(CDC)的免疫实践咨询委员会(ACIP)改变了对普遍出生疫苗接种的政策,但该研究强调了考虑非母系接触的重要性。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Not Just Moms Who Put Unvaccinated Infants at Risk of Hepatitis B, Study Shows - MedPage Today

News Brief
A study presented at the IDWeek annual meeting in 2026 revealed that among children under 5, 1.3% lived in households with active hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection, with 58.2% of these children exposed to HBV through nonmaternal contacts. The research, led by Karen Jacobson, MD, MPH, of the Kaiser Permanente Vaccine Study Center, highlighted that even with high vaccination rates, skipping the universal birth dose of HBV vaccine could increase the risk of preventable infections, liver cancers, and deaths. The study found that 4.2% of children exposed to HBV by nonmaternal household contacts were unvaccinated by age 30 days. While 95% of birth-exposed infants were tested for HBV, only 3.6% of children with household exposures were tested. Jacobson emphasized that nonmaternal exposures should not be overlooked, as they can lead to chronic infections with lifelong consequences. The CDC's Advisory Committee on Immunization Practices (ACIP) voted to stop recommending a universal birth dose for infants born to mothers who test negative for HBV, instead advocating for shared decision-making. Critics warned that this change could result in a significant rise in HBV infections, with a modeling study suggesting an 83% increase in chronic infections. The study also noted limitations, including potential inaccuracies in address data and underestimation of HBV-positive household contacts not affiliated with KPNC.
Original source text (English)

Meeting Coverage > IDWeek Not Just Moms Who Put Unvaccinated Infants at Risk of Hepatitis B, Study Shows — The findings undermine the CDC's recommendations against universal birth dose vaccination by Terrence Rudd, Staff Writer, MedPage Today October 21, 2026 • 3 min read Add MedPage Today on Google MedpageToday WASHINGTON -- For young children, exposure to hepatitis B virus (HBV) mostly came from someone other than their mother in the household, according to a retrospective cohort study. Among over 158,000 children younger than 5 years, 1.3% lived in a household that had a person with active HBV infection, with 58.2% of those children exposed via contacts who were not their mothers versus 41.8% who were exposed via their mothers, reported Karen Jacobson, MD, MPH, of the Kaiser Permanente Vaccine Study Center in Pleasanton, California, at the IDWeek opens in a new tab or windowannual meeting. "Our findings show that even if it is possible to accurately identify every single maternal infection, this would not account for potential exposure to an infected father, grandparent, or another person living in the household -- and of course anyone that they might come into close contact with outside the household," Jacobson said during a press briefing before the meeting. "Transmission of hepatitis B to children from nonmaternal sources is currently quite rare in the U.S. due to the high vaccination rates," she noted. "But if rates decrease, more infants could be at risk." A universal birth dose of the HBV vaccine protects babiesopens in a new tab or window from such household exposures. Despite that, the CDC's Advisory Committee on Immunization Practices (ACIP) in December voted to reverse more than three decades of U.S. vaccine policy and stop recommending a universal doseopens in a new tab or window of the HBV vaccine at birth for infants born to mothers who test negative for hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg). ACIP continues to recommend that infants born to mothers who test positive or who are unscreened for HBsAg receive a birth dose within 12 hours of delivery. When a mother tests negative, however, ACIP called for shared decision-making about HBV vaccination birth doses. Critics have warned that skipping the universal birth dose will cause preventable acute and chronic HBV infectionsopens in a new tab or window, liver cancers, and deaths, and add millions of dollars in additional healthcare costs. A recent modeling studyopens in a new tab or window suggested that the proposed shared decision-making approach would increase chronic HBV infections by 83%. In babies who don't get a birth dose, ACIP recommended delaying the first dose of the three-dose series to at least age 2 months. But Jacobson's study showed that 4.2% of children exposed to HBV by a nonmaternal household contact were unvaccinated by age 30 days -- within that 2-month window. Medical News from Around the Web 1 Live Science A fighter pilot had unexplained headaches for over a year, and the cause was hiding in plain sight — Diagnostic dilemma opens in a new tab or window 2 Harvard Health Radiofrequency ablation for back pain - Harvard Health opens in a new tab or window 3 Scientific American Can AI predict whether you’ll die from cancer from just an image of your face? opens in a new tab or window 4 Clinical Advisor Epilepsy Strongly Linked to Young-Onset Dementia opens in a new tab or window 5 NPR Expecting a baby? 5 tips to help you prepare for pregnancy costs opens in a new tab or window ACIP's latest recommendations "are really focusing on that maternal exposure, and I think it's important that other sources of potential exposure are not ignored," Jacobson said. While 95% of birth-exposed infants were tested for HBV, only 3.6% of children with household exposures were tested. All birth-exposed children were vaccinated within 7 days of birth. Children with household exposure were more likely (93.5%) than those who weren't exposed (86.7%) to be vaccinated. "This suggests that some families or healthcare providers did recognize the potential risk associated with household exposure," Jacobson pointed out. Jacobson and team analyzed laboratory and vaccination data through March for children younger than 5 years who were members of Kaiser Permanente Northern California (KPNC). Children were linked via addresses to birth mothers and households with at least one other KPNC member. The 158,169 children lived in 131,800 households, of which 1,689 had a person with active HBV. Nonmaternal household contacts were older (median age 44) than maternal contacts (median age 38), and 76.5% were male. Because children aren't routinely screened for HBV, Jacobson's team couldn't determine whether any of the household-exposed children developed HBV infections. The study's 1.3% household exposure rate may sound low, Jacobson noted, but "it's important to remember that this is likely an underestimate," and "when infection occurs in infancy, it's highly likely to result in chronic infection with lifelong sequelae." Study limitations included potential inaccurate addresses and underestimates if household contacts with HBV weren't KPNC members. Please enable JavaScript to view the comments powered by Disqus. Time
The study's findings were reported at the IDWeek annual meeting in 2026, with the publication date being October 21, 2026. The data analyzed was through March 2026.
Location and population
The study focused on children younger than 5 years in Kaiser Permanente Northern California (KPNC), specifically those living in households with at least one person with active HBV infection.
Event
A retrospective cohort study showed that among children under 5, 1.3% lived in households with active HBV infection, with 58.2% of those children exposed via nonmaternal contacts. The study was presented by Karen Jacobson, MD, MPH, at the IDWeek annual meeting.
Scale, impact and risk
Among children exposed to HBV by nonmaternal household contacts, 4.2% were unvaccinated by age 30 days. The study highlighted that household exposure could lead to chronic HBV infections, liver cancers, and deaths, especially if vaccination rates decrease. It also noted that 95% of birth-exposed infants were tested for HBV, while only 3.6% of children with household exposures were tested.
Response, status and uncertainty
The study analyzed laboratory and vaccination data through March 2026. It acknowledged limitations such as potential inaccurate addresses and underestimation of household contacts with HBV who were not KPNC members. The CDC's Advisory Committee on Immunization Practices (ACIP) reversed its policy on universal birth dose vaccination, but the study emphasized the importance of considering nonmaternal exposures.
